




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

vinayapiṭakai

sāratthadīpanī-ṭīkā (paṭhamau bhāgau)

ganthārambhakathā

mahākāruṇikaṃ  buddhaṃ, dhammañ‍ca vimalaṃ varaṃ|

vandai ariyasaṅghañ‍ca, dakkhiṇaiyyaṃ niraṅgaṇaṃ||

uḷārapuñ‍ñataijaina, katvā sattuvimaddanaṃ|

pattaraj‍jābhisaikaina, sāsanuj‍jautanatthinā||

nissāya  sīhaḷindaina, yaṃ parak‍kamabāhunā|

katvā nikāyasāmaggiṃ, sāsanaṃ suvisaudhitaṃ||

kassapaṃ taṃ mahāthairaṃ, saṅghassa pariṇāyakaṃ|

dīpasmiṃ tambapaṇṇimhi, sāsanaudayakārakaṃ||

paṭipattiparādhīnaṃ, sadārañ‍ñanivāsinaṃ|

pākaṭaṃ gaganai canda-maṇḍalaṃ viya sāsanai||

saṅghassa pitaraṃ vandai, vinayai suvisāradaṃ|

yaṃ nissāya vasantauhaṃ, vuddhippattausmi sāsanai||

anuthairaṃ mahāpuñ‍ñaṃ, sumaidhaṃ sutivissutaṃ|

avikhaṇḍitasīlādi-parisuddhaguṇaudayaṃ||

bahussutaṃ satimantaṃ, dantaṃ santaṃ samāhitaṃ|

namāmi sirasā dhīraṃ, garuṃ mai gaṇavācakaṃ||

āgatāgamatak‍kaisu, saddasatthanayañ‍ñusu|

yassantaivāsibhikkhūsu, sāsanaṃ suppatiṭṭhitaṃ||

vinayaṭṭhakathāyāhaṃ, līnasāratthadīpaniṃ|

karissāmi suviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, paripuṇṇamanākulaṃ||

paurāṇaihi kataṃ yaṃ tu, līnatthassa pakāsanaṃ|

na taṃ sabbattha bhikkhūnaṃ, atthaṃ sādhaiti sabbasau||

duviñ‍ñaiyyasabhāvāya, sīhaḷāya niruttiyā|

gaṇṭhipadaisvanaikaisu, likhitaṃ kiñ‍ci katthaci||

māgadhikāya  bhāsāya, ārabhitvāpi kainaci|

bhāsantaraihi sammissaṃ, likhitaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva ca||

asāraganthabhāraupi, tatthaiva bahu dissati|

ākulañ‍ca kataṃ yattha, suviñ‍ñaiyyampi atthatau||

tatau aparipuṇṇaina, tādisainaittha sabbasau|

kathamatthaṃ vijānanti, nānādaisanivāsinau||

bhāsantaraṃ tatau hitvā, sāramādāya sabbasau|

anākulaṃ karissāmi, paripuṇṇavinicchayanti||

ganthārambhakathāvaṇṇanā

vinayasaṃvaṇṇanārambhai ratanattayaṃ namassitukāmau tassa visiṭṭhaguṇayaugasandassanatthaṃ ‘‘yau kappakauṭīhipī’’tiādimāha| visiṭṭhaguṇayaugaina hi vandanārahabhāvau, vandanārahai ca katā vandanā yathādhippaitamatthaṃ sādhaiti| aittha ca saṃvaṇṇanārambhai ratanattayapaṇāmakaraṇappayaujanaṃ tattha tattha bahudhā papañ‍cainti ācariyā| tathā hi vaṇṇayanti –

‘‘saṃvaṇṇanārambhai ratanattayavandanā saṃvaṇṇaitabbassa dhammassa pabhavanissayavisuddhipaṭivaidanatthaṃ, taṃ pana dhammasaṃvaṇṇanāsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ bahumānuppādanatthaṃ, taṃ sammadaiva taisaṃ uggahaṇadhāraṇādik‍kamaladdhabbāya sammāpaṭipattiyā sabbahitasukhanipphādanatthaṃ| atha vā maṅgalabhāvatau, sabbakiriyāsu pubbakic‍cabhāvatau, paṇḍitaihi samācaritabhāvatau, āyatiṃ paraisaṃ diṭṭhānugatiāpaj‍janatau ca saṃvaṇṇanāyaṃ ratanattayapaṇāmakiriyā’’ti|

mayaṃ pana idhādhippaitamaiva payaujanaṃ dassayissāma| tasmā saṃvaṇṇanārambhai ratanattayapaṇāmakaraṇaṃ yathāpaṭiñ‍ñātasaṃvaṇṇanāya anantarāyaina parisamāpanatthanti vaiditabbaṃ| idamaiva hi payaujanaṃ ācariyaina idhādhippaitaṃ| tathā hi vakkhati –

‘‘ic‍caivamac‍cantanamassanaiyyaṃ,

namassamānau ratanattayaṃ yaṃ|

puñ‍ñābhisandaṃ vipulaṃ alatthaṃ,

tassānubhāvaina hatantarāyau’’ti||

ratanattayapaṇāmakaraṇaina caittha yathāpaṭiñ‍ñātasaṃvaṇṇanāya anantarāyaina parisamāpanaṃ ratanattayapūjāya pañ‍ñāpāṭavabhāvatau, tāya pañ‍ñāpāṭavañ‍ca rāgādimalavidhamanatau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hautī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 11.11)|

tasmā ratanattayapūjanaina vikkhālitamalāya pañ‍ñāya pāṭavasiddhi|

atha vā ratanattayapūjanassa pañ‍ñāpadaṭṭhānasamādhihaituttā pañ‍ñāpāṭavaṃ| vuttañhi tassa samādhihaituttaṃ –

‘‘aivaṃ ujugatacittau khau, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyau passambhati, passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidiyati, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyatī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 11.11.)|

samādhissa ca pañ‍ñāya padaṭṭhānabhāvau vuttauyaiva ‘‘samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.99; mi॰ pa॰ 2.1.14)| tatau aivaṃ paṭubhūtāya pañ‍ñāya paṭiñ‍ñāmahattakataṃ  khaidamabhibhuyya anantarāyaina saṃvaṇṇanaṃ samāpayissati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘anantarāyaina parisamāpanattha’’nti|

atha vā ratanattayapūjāya āyuvaṇṇasukhabalavaḍḍhanatau anantarāyaina parisamāpanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| ratanattayapaṇāmaina hi āyuvaṇṇasukhabalāni vaḍḍhanti| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘abhivādanasīlissa, nic‍caṃ vuḍḍhāpacāyinau|

cattārau dhammā vaḍḍhanti, āyu vaṇṇau sukhaṃ bala’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 109)|

tatau āyuvaṇṇasukhabalavuḍḍhiyā hautaiva kāriyaniṭṭhānamiti vuttaṃ ‘‘anantarāyaina parisamāpanattha’’nti|

atha vā ratanattayagāravassa paṭibhānāparihānāvahattā| aparihānāvahañhi tīsupi ratanaisu gāravaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘sattimai, bhikkhavai, aparihāniyā dhammā| katamai satta? satthugāravatā dhammagāravatā saṅghagāravatā sikkhāgāravatā samādhigāravatā kalyāṇamittatā sauvacassatā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 7.34)|

hautaiva ca tatau paṭibhānāparihānaina yathāpaṭiñ‍ñātaparisamāpanaṃ|

atha vā pasādavatthūsu pūjāya puñ‍ñātisayabhāvatau| vuttañhi tassa puñ‍ñātisayattaṃ –

‘‘pūjārahai pūjayatau, buddhai yadiva sāvakai|

papañ‍casamatik‍kantai, tiṇṇasaukapariddavai||

‘‘tai tādisai pūjayatau, nibbutai akutaubhayai|

na sak‍kā puñ‍ñaṃ saṅkhātuṃ, imaittamapi kainacī’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 195-196; apa॰ thaira 1.10.1-2)|

puñ‍ñātisayau ca yathādhippaitaparisamāpanupāyau| yathāha –

‘‘aisa daivamanussānaṃ, sabbakāmadadau nidhi|

yaṃ yadaivābhipatthainti, sabbamaitaina labbhatī’’ti|| (khu॰ pā॰ 8.10)|

upāyaisu  ca paṭipan‍nassa hautaiva kāriyaniṭṭhānaṃ| ratanattayapūjā hi niratisayapuñ‍ñakkhaittasaṃbuddhiyā aparimaiyyappabhavau puñ‍ñātisayauti bahuvidhantarāyaipi  laukasan‍nivāsai antarāyanibandhanasakalasaṃkilaisaviddhaṃsanāya pahauti, bhayādiupaddavañ‍ca nivāraiti| tasmā suvuttaṃ ‘‘saṃvaṇṇanārambhai ratanattayapaṇāmakaraṇaṃ yathāpaṭiñ‍ñātasaṃvaṇṇanāya anantarāyaina parisamāpanatthanti vaiditabba’’nti|

aivaṃ pana sappayaujanaṃ ratanattayavandanaṃ kattukāmau paṭhamaṃ tāva bhagavatau vandanaṃ kātuṃ tammūlakattā saisaratanānaṃ ‘‘yau kappa…pai॰… mahākāruṇikassa tassā’’ti āha| aittha pana yassā daisanāya saṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau, sā yasmā karuṇāppadhānā, na suttantadaisanā viya karuṇāpañ‍ñāppadhānā, nāpi abhidhammadaisanā viya pañ‍ñāppadhānā, tasmā karuṇāppadhānamaiva bhagavatau thaumanaṃ āraddhaṃ| aisā hi ācariyassa pakati, yadidaṃ ārambhānurūpathaumanā| tainaiva suttantadaisanāya saṃvaṇṇanārambhai ‘‘karuṇāsītalahadayaṃ, pañ‍ñāpaj‍jautavihatamauhatama’’nti karuṇāpañ‍ñāppadhānaṃ, abhidhammadaisanāya saṃvaṇṇanārambhai ‘‘karuṇā viya sattaisu, pañ‍ñā yassa mahaisinau’’ti pañ‍ñāppadhānañ‍ca thaumanaṃ āraddhaṃ| karuṇāpañ‍ñāppadhānā hi suttantadaisanā taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ āsayānusayādhimutticariyādibhaidaparicchindanasamatthāya pañ‍ñāya sattaisu ca mahākaruṇāya tattha sātisayappavattitau| suttantadaisanāya hi mahākaruṇāsamāpattibahulau vainaiyyasantānaisu tadajjhāsayānulaumaina gambhīramatthapadaṃ patiṭṭhāpaisi| abhidhammadaisanā ca kaivalaṃ pañ‍ñāppadhānā paramatthadhammānaṃ yathāsabhāvapaṭivaidhasamatthāya pañ‍ñāya tattha sātisayappavattitau|

vinayadaisanā pana āsayādinirapaikkhaṃ kaivalaṃ karuṇāya pākatikasattainapi asautabbārahaṃ suṇantau apucchitabbārahaṃ pucchantau avattabbārahañ‍ca vadantau bhagavā sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñapaisīti karuṇāppadhānā| tathā hi uk‍kaṃsapariyantagatahirauttappaupi bhagavā laukiyasādhujanaihipi pariharitabbāni ‘‘sikharaṇīsī’’tiādīni vacanāni yathāparādhañ‍ca garahavacanāni vinayapiṭakadaisanāya mahākaruṇāsañ‍cauditamānasau mahāparisamajjhai abhāsi, taṃtaṃsikkhāpadapañ‍ñattikāraṇāpaikkhāya vairañ‍jādīsu sārīrikañ‍ca khaidamanubhausi| tasmā kiñ‍cāpi bhūmantarapac‍cayākārasamayantarakathānaṃ viya vinayapañ‍ñattiyāpi samuṭṭhāpikā pañ‍ñā anañ‍ñasādhāraṇatāya atisayakic‍cavatī, karuṇāya kic‍caṃ pana tataupi adhikanti  karuṇāppadhānā vinayadaisanā| karuṇābyāpārādhikatāya hi daisanāya karuṇāppadhānatā| tasmā ārambhānurūpaṃ karuṇāppadhānamaiva aittha thaumanaṃ katanti vaiditabbaṃ|

karuṇāggahaṇaina  ca aparimaiyyappabhāvā sabbaipi buddhaguṇā saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbā taṃmūlakattā saisabuddhaguṇānaṃ| mahākaruṇāya vā chasu asādhāraṇañāṇaisu añ‍ñatarattā taṃsahacaritasaisāsādhāraṇañāṇānampi gahaṇasabbhāvatau sabbaipi buddhaguṇā nayatau dassitāva haunti| aisauyaiva hi niravasaisatau buddhaguṇānaṃ dassanupāyau yadidaṃ nayaggāhau| añ‍ñathā kau nāma samatthau bhagavatau guṇai anupadaṃ niravasaisatau dassaituṃ| tainaivāha –

‘‘buddhaupi buddhassa bhaṇaiyya vaṇṇaṃ,

kappampi cai añ‍ñamabhāsamānau|

khīyaitha kappau ciradīghamantarai,

vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassā’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.304; 3.141; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.425)|

tainaiva ca āyasmatā sāriputtatthairainapi buddhaguṇaparicchaidanaṃ patianuyuttaina ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ti paṭikkhipitvā ‘‘apica mai, bhantai, dhammanvayau viditau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.146) vuttaṃ| tasmā ‘‘yau kappakauṭīhipī’’tiādinā karuṇāmukhaina saṅkhaipatau sakalasabbañ‍ñuguṇaihi bhagavantaṃ abhitthavīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ayamaittha samudāyatthau|

ayaṃ pana avayavatthau – yauti aniyamavacanaṃ| tassa ‘‘nāthau’’ti iminā sambandhau| ‘‘kappakauṭīhipī’’tiādinā pana yāya karuṇāya sau ‘‘mahākāruṇikau’’ti vuc‍cati, tassā vasaina kappakauṭigaṇanāyapi appamaiyyaṃ kālaṃ laukahitatthāya atiduk‍karaṃ karauntassa bhagavatau dukkhānubhavanaṃ dassaiti| karuṇāya balainaiva hi sau bhagavā hatthagatampi nibbānaṃ pahāya saṃsārapaṅkai nimuggaṃ sattanikāyaṃ tatau samuddharaṇatthaṃ cintaitumpi asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ nayanajīvitaputtabhariyadānādikaṃ atiduk‍karamakāsi| kappakauṭīhipi appamaiyyaṃ kālanti kappakauṭigaṇanāyapi ‘‘aittakā kappakauṭiyau’’ti pamaituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyaṃ kālaṃ, kappakauṭigaṇanavasainapi paricchinditumasak‍kuṇaiyyattā aparicchin‍nāni kappasatasahassādhikāni  cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyānīti vuttaṃ hauti| kappakauṭivasainaiva hi sau kālau appamaiyyau, asaṅkhyaiyyavasaina pana paricchin‍nauyaiva| ‘‘kappakauṭīhipī’’ti apisaddau kappakauṭivasainapi tāva pamaituṃ na sak‍kā, pagaiva vassagaṇanāyāti dassaiti| ‘‘appamaiyyaṃ kāla’’nti ac‍cantasaṃyaugai upayaugavacanaṃ ‘‘māsamadhītai, divasaṃ caratī’’tiādīsu viya| karauntau atiduk‍karānīti pañ‍camahāparic‍cāgādīni atiduk‍karāni karauntau| aivamatiduk‍karāni  karauntau kiṃ vindīti cai? khaidaṃ gatau, kāyikaṃ khaidamupagatau, parissamaṃ pattauti atthau, dukkhamanubhavīti vuttaṃ hauti| dukkhañhi khij‍jati sahitumasak‍kuṇaiyyanti ‘‘khaidau’’ti vuc‍cati| laukahitāyāti ‘‘anamataggai saṃsārai vaṭṭadukkhaina ac‍cantapīḷitaṃ sattalaukaṃ tamhā dukkhatau maucaitvā nibbānasukhabhāgiyaṃ karissāmī’’ti aivaṃ sattalaukassa hitakaraṇatthāyāti atthau| assa ca ‘‘atiduk‍karāni karauntau’’ti iminā sambandhau| laukahitāya khaidaṃ gatauti yaujanāyapi natthi dausau| mahāgaṇṭhipadaipi hi ‘‘atiduk‍karāni karauntau khaidaṃ gatau, kimatthanti cai? laukahitāyā’’ti vuttaṃ|

yaṃ pana aivaṃ yaujanaṃ asambhāvaintaina kainaci vuttaṃ ‘‘na hi bhagavā laukahitāya saṃsāradukkhamanubhavati| na hi kassaci dukkhānubhavanaṃ laukassa upakāraṃ āvahatī’’ti, taṃ tassa matimattaṃ| aivaṃ yaujanāyapi atiduk‍karāni karauntassa bhagavatau dukkhānubhavanaṃ laukahitakaraṇatthāyāti ayamatthau viñ‍ñāyati, na tu dukkhānubhavanainaiva laukahitasiddhīti| paṭhamaṃ vuttayaujanāyapi hi na duk‍karakaraṇamattaina laukahitasiddhi| na hi duk‍karaṃ karauntau kañ‍ci sattaṃ maggaphalādīsu patiṭṭhāpaiti, atha khau tādisaṃ atiduk‍karaṃ katvā sabbañ‍ñubhāvaṃ sacchikatvā niyyānikadhammadaisanāya maggaphalādīsu sattai patiṭṭhāpaintau laukassa hitaṃ sādhaiti|

kāmañ‍caittha sattasaṅkhārabhājanavasaina tividhau laukau, hitakaraṇassa pana adhippaitattā taṃvisayassaiva sattalaukassa vasaina atthau gahaitabbau| sau hi laukīyanti aittha puñ‍ñapāpāni taṃvipākau cāti ‘‘laukau’’ti vuc‍cati| katthaci pana ‘‘sanarāmaralaukagaru’’ntiādīsu samūhatthaupi laukasaddau samudāyavasaina laukīyati pañ‍ñāpīyatīti| yaṃ  panaittha kainaci vuttaṃ ‘‘iminā sattalaukañ‍ca jātilaukañ‍ca saṅgaṇhāti, tasmā tassa sattalaukassa idhalaukaparalaukahitaṃ, atik‍kantaparalaukānaṃ vā ucchin‍nalaukasamudayānaṃ idha jātilaukai aukāsalaukai vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārasaṅkhātañ‍ca hitaṃ sampiṇḍaitvā laukassa, laukānaṃ, laukai vā hitanti sarūpaikasaisaṃ katvā laukahitamic‍caivāhā’’ti, na taṃ sāratau pac‍caitabbaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārasaṅkhaātahitassapi sattalaukavisayattā, sattalaukaggahaṇainaiva ucchin‍namūlānaṃ khīṇāsavānampi saṅgahitattā|

sabbattha ‘‘kainacī’’ti vuttai ‘‘vajirabuddhiṭīkākārainā’’ti gahaitabbaṃ| ‘‘mahāgaṇṭhipadai’’ti vā ‘‘majjhimagaṇṭhipadai’’ti vā ‘‘cūḷagaṇṭhipadai’’ti vā vuttai ‘‘sīhaḷagaṇṭhipadaisū’’ti  gahaitabbaṃ| kaivalaṃ ‘‘gaṇṭhipadai’’ti vuttai ‘‘māgadhabhāsāya likhitai gaṇṭhipadai’’ti gahaitabbaṃ|

nāthauti laukapaṭisaraṇau, laukasāmī laukanāyakauti vuttaṃ hauti| tathā hi sabbānatthapaahārapubbaṅgamāya niravasaisahitasukhavidhānatapparāya niratisayāya payaugasampattiyā sadaivamanussāya pajāya ac‍cantupakāritāya aparimitanirupamappabhāvaguṇavisaisasamaṅgitāya ca sabbasattuttamau bhagavā aparimāṇāsu laukadhātūsu aparimāṇānaṃ sattānaṃ aikapaṭisaraṇau patiṭṭhā| atha vā nāthatīti nāthau, vainaiyyānaṃ hitasukhaṃ maittāyanavasaina āsīsati patthaitīti atthau| atha vā nāthati vainaiyyagatai kilaisai upatāpaitīti atthau, nāthatīti vā yācatīti atthau| bhagavā hi ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kālaina kālaṃ attasampattiṃ pac‍cavaikkhaiyyā’’tiādinā (a॰ ni॰ 8.7) sattānaṃ taṃ taṃ hitapaṭipattiṃ yācitvāpi karuṇāya samussāhitau tai tattha niyaujaiti| paramaina vā cittissariyaina saman‍nāgatau sabbasattai īsati abhibhavatīti paramissarau bhagavā ‘‘nāthau’’ti vuc‍cati| sabbaupi cāyamatthau saddasatthānusāratau vaiditabbau|

mahākāruṇikassāti yau karuṇāya kampitahadayattā laukahitatthaṃ atiduk‍karakiriyāya anaikappakāraṃ tādisaṃ saṃsāradukkhamanubhavitvā āgatau, tassa mahākāruṇikassāti atthau| tattha kiratīti  karuṇā, paradukkhaṃ vikkhipati apanaitīti atthau| dukkhitaisu vā kirīyati pasārīyatīti karuṇā| atha vā kiṇātīti karuṇā, paradukkhai sati kāruṇikaṃ hiṃsati vibādhaiti, vināsaiti vā parassa dukkhanti atthau| paradukkhai sati sādhūnaṃ kampanaṃ hadayakhaidaṃ karautīti vā karuṇā| atha vā kamiti sukhaṃ, taṃ rundhatīti karuṇā| aisā hi paradukkhāpanayanakāmatālakkhaṇā attasukhanirapaikkhatāya kāruṇikānaṃ sukhaṃ rundhati vibādhaitīti| karuṇāya niyuttauti kāruṇikau yathā ‘‘dauvārikau’’ti| yathā hi dvāraṭṭhānatau añ‍ñattha vattamānaupi dvārapaṭibaddhajīvikau purisau dvārānativattavuttitāya dvārai niyuttauti ‘‘dauvārikau’’ti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ bhagavā maittādivasaina karuṇāvihāratau añ‍ñattha vattamānaupi karuṇānativattavuttitāya karuṇāya niyuttauti ‘‘kāruṇikau’’ti vuc‍cati| mahābhinīhāratau paṭṭhāya hi yāva mahāparinibbānā laukahitatthamaiva laukanāthā tiṭṭhanti| mahantau kāruṇikauti mahākāruṇikau| satipi bhagavatau tadañ‍ñaguṇānampi vasaina mahantabhāvai kāruṇikasaddasan‍nidhānaina vuttattā karuṇāvasainaittha mahantabhāvau vaiditabbau yathā ‘‘mahāvaiyyākaraṇau’’ti| aivañ‍ca katvā ‘‘mahākāruṇikassā’’ti iminā padaina puggalādhiṭṭhānaina satthu mahākaruṇā vuttā hauti|

atha  vā karuṇā karuṇāyanaṃ sīlaṃ pakati aitassāti kāruṇikau, pathavīphassādayau viya kakkhaḷaphusanādisabhāvā karuṇāyanasabhāvau sabhāvabhūtakaruṇauti atthau| saisaṃ purimasadisamaiva| atha vā mahāvisayatāya mahānubhāvatāya mahābalatāya ca mahatī karuṇāti mahākaruṇā| bhagavatau hi karuṇā niravasaisaisu sattaisu pavattati, pavattamānā ca anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā pavattati, diṭṭhadhammikādibhaidañ‍ca mahantamaiva sattānaṃ hitasukhaṃ aikantatau nipphādaiti, mahākaruṇāya niyuttauti mahākāruṇikauti sabbaṃ vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| atha vā mahatī pasatthā karuṇā assa atthīti mahākāruṇikau| pūjāvacanau haittha mahantasaddau ‘‘mahāpurisau’’tiādīsu viya| pasatthā ca bhagavatau karuṇā mahākaruṇāsamāpattivasainapi pavattitau anañ‍ñasādhāraṇattāti|

aivaṃ  karuṇāmukhaina saṅkhaipatau sakalasabbañ‍ñuguṇaihi bhagavantaṃ thaumaitvā idāni saddhammaṃ thaumaituṃ ‘‘asambudha’’ntiādimāha| tattha asambudhanti pubbakālakiriyāniddaisau, tassa asambujjhantau appaṭivijjhantauti atthau, yathāsabhāvaṃ appaṭivijjhanatauti vuttaṃ hauti| haituatthau haittha antasaddau ‘‘paṭhantau nisīdatī’’tiādīsu viya| yanti pubbakālakiriyāya aniyamatau kammaniddaisau| buddhanisaivitanti tassa visaisanaṃ| tattha buddhasaddassa tāva ‘‘bujjhitā sac‍cānīti buddhau, baudhaitā pajāyāti buddhau’’tiādinā (mahāni॰ 192) niddaisanayaina atthau vaiditabbau| atha vā savāsanāya añ‍ñāṇaniddāya ac‍cantavigamatau, buddhiyā vā vikasitabhāvatau buddhavāti buddhau jāgaraṇavikasanatthavasaina| atha vā kassacipi ñaiyyadhammassa anavabuddhassa abhāvaina ñaiyyavisaisassa kammabhāvaina aggahaṇatau kammavacanicchāya abhāvaina avagamanatthavasainaiva kattuniddaisau labbhatīti buddhavāti buddhau| atthatau pana pāramitāparibhāvitau sayambhūñāṇaina saha vāsanāya vihataviddhaṃsitaniravasaisakilaisau mahākaruṇāsabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇādiaparimaiyyaguṇagaṇādhārau khandhasantānau buddhau| yathāha ‘‘buddhauti yau sau bhagavā sayambhū anācariyakau pubbai ananussutaisu dhammaisu sāmaṃ sac‍cāni abhisambujjhi, tattha ca sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattau balaisu ca vasībhāva’’nti (mahāni॰ 192; cūḷani॰ pārāyanatthutigāthāniddaisa 97; paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.161)| taina aivaṃ nirupamappabhāvaina buddhaina nisaivitaṃ gaucarāsaivanābhāvanāsaivanāhi yathārahaṃ nisaivitaṃ anubhūtanti atthau|

tattha nibbānaṃ gaucarāsaivanāvasainaiva nisaivitaṃ, maggau pana attanā bhāvitau ca bhāvanāsaivanāvasaina saivitau, paraihi uppāditāni pana maggaphalāni caitaupariyañāṇādinā yadā parijānāti, attanā uppāditāni vā pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaina paricchindati, tadā gaucarāsaivanāvasainapi  saivitāni hauntiyaiva| aittha ca pariyattidhammassapi pariyāyatau dhammaggahaṇaina gahaṇai sati saupi daisanāsammasanañāṇagaucaratāya gaucarāsaivanāya saivitauti sak‍kā gahaituṃ| ‘‘abhidhammanayasamuddaṃ adhigacchati, tīṇi piṭakāni sammasī’’ti ca aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttattā pariyattidhammassapi sacchikiriyāya sammasanapariyāyau labbhatīti yaṃ asambudhaṃ asambujjhantau asacchikarauntauti atthasambhavatau saupi idha vuttau aivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| tampi  ca appaṭivijjhantau bhavābhavaṃ gacchati, pariñ‍ñātadhammavinayau pana tadatthapaṭipattiyā sammāpaṭipan‍nau na cirassaiva dukkhassantaṃ karissati| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘yau imasmiṃ dhammavinayai, appamattau vihassati|

pahāya jātisaṃsāraṃ, dukkhassantaṃ karissatī’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ 2.185; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.185)|

aittha ca kiñ‍cāpi maggaphalanibbānāni pac‍caikabuddhabuddhasāvakaihipi gaucarāsaivanādinā saivitāni haunti, tathāpi uk‍kaṭṭhaparicchaidavasaina ‘‘buddhanisaivita’’nti vuttaṃ| kainaci pana buddhasaddassa sāmañ‍ñatau buddhānubuddhapac‍caikabuddhānampi aitthaiva saṅgahau vuttau|

bhavābhavanti aparakālakiriyāya kammaniddaisau, bhavatau bhavanti atthau| atha vā bhavābhavanti sugatiduggativasaina hīnapaṇītavasaina ca khuddakaṃ mahantañ‍ca bhavanti atthau| vuddhatthaupi hi a-kārau dissati ‘‘asaikkhā dhammā’’tiādīsu viya| tasmā abhavauti mahābhavau vuc‍cati| atha vā bhavauti vuddhi, abhavauti hāni| bhavauti vā sassatadiṭṭhi, abhavauti ucchaidadiṭṭhi| vuttappakārau bhavau ca abhavau ca bhavābhavau| taṃ bhavābhavaṃ| gacchatīti aparakālakiriyāniddaisau| jīvalaukauti sattalaukau| jīvaggahaṇaina hi saṅkhārabhājanalaukaṃ nivattaiti tassa bhavābhavagamanāsambhavatau| namau atthūti pāṭhasaisau daṭṭhabbau|

avij‍jādikilaisajālaviddhaṃsinauti dhammavisaisanaṃ| tattha avindiyaṃ vindatīti avij‍jā| pūraituṃ ayuttaṭṭhaina kāyaduc‍caritādi avindiyaṃ nāma, aladdhabbanti atthau| tabbiparītatau kāyasucaritādi vindiyaṃ nāma, taṃ vindiyaṃ na vindatīti vā avij‍jā, khandhānaṃ rāsaṭṭhaṃ, āyatanānaṃ āyatanaṭṭhaṃ, dhātūnaṃ suñ‍ñataṭṭhaṃ, indriyānaṃ adhipatiyaṭṭhaṃ, sac‍cānaṃ tathaṭṭhaṃ aviditaṃ karautīti vā avij‍jā, dukkhādīnaṃ pīḷanādivasaina vuttaṃ catubbidhaṃ atthaṃ aviditaṃ karautītipi avij‍jā, antavirahitai saṃsārai sabbayaunigatibhavaviñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāvāsaisu sattai javāpaitīti  vā avij‍jā, paramatthatau avij‍jamānaisupi itthipurisādīsu javati, vij‍jamānaisupi khandhādīsu na javatīti vā avij‍jā| sā ādi yaisaṃ taṇhādīnaṃ tai avij‍jādayau, taiyaiva  kilissanti aitaihi sattāti kilaisā, taiyaiva ca sattānaṃ bandhanaṭṭhaina jālasadisāti jālaṃ, taṃ viddhaṃsaiti sabbasau vināsaiti sīlaināti avij‍jādikilaisajālaviddhaṃsī| nanu caittha sapariyattikau navalaukuttaradhammau adhippaitau, tattha ca maggauyaiva kilaisai viddhaṃsaiti, naitaraiti cai? vuc‍catai| maggassapi nibbānamāgamma kilaisaviddhaṃsanatau nibbānampi kilaisai viddhaṃsaiti nāma, maggassa kilaisaviddhaṃsanakic‍caṃ phalaina nipphan‍nanti phalampi ‘‘kilaisaviddhaṃsī’’ti vuc‍cati| pariyattidhammaupi kilaisaviddhaṃsanassa pac‍cayattā ‘‘kilaisaviddhaṃsī’’ti vattumarahatīti na kauci dausau|

dhammavarassa tassāti pubbai aniyamitassa niyamavacanaṃ| tattha yathānusiṭṭhaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānai catūsu apāyaisu apatamānai dhāraitīti dhammau|

‘‘yai kaici dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatāsai, na tai gamissanti apāyabhūmiṃ|

pahāya mānusaṃ daihaṃ, daivakāyaṃ paripūraissantī’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ 2.332; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.37) –

hi vuttaṃ| saṃsāradukkhai vā apatamānai katvā dhāraitīti dhammau maggaphaluppattiyā sattakkhattuparamatādivasaina saṃsārassa paricchin‍nattā| apāyādinibbattakakilaisaviddhaṃsanañ‍caittha dhāraṇaṃ| aivañ‍ca katvā ariyamaggau tassa tadatthasiddhihaitutāya nibbānañ‍cāti ubhayamaiva nippariyāyatau dhāraiti, ariyaphalaṃ pana taṃsamucchin‍nakilaisapaṭippassambhanaina tadanuguṇatāya, pariyattidhammau tadadhigamahaitutāyāti ubhayaṃ pariyāyatau dhāraitīti vaiditabbaṃ| vuttappakārau dhammauyaiva attanau uttaritarābhāvaina varau pavarau anuttarauti dhammavarau, tassa dhammavarassa namau atthūti sambandhau| aittāvatā caittha amhaihi sāratthau pakāsitau| yaṃ panaittha kainaci papañ‍citaṃ, amhaihi ca idha na dassitaṃ, na taṃ sāratau pac‍caitabbaṃ| itau paraisupi aivamaiva daṭṭhabbaṃ| tasmā itau paṭṭhāya aittakampi avatvā sāratthamaiva dassayissāma| yattha pana kainaci ac‍cantaviruddhaṃ likhitaṃ, tampi katthaci dassayissāma| aittha ca ‘‘avij‍jādikilaisajālaviddhaṃsinau’’ti aitaina svākkhātatādīhi dhammaṃ thaumaiti, ‘‘dhammavarassā’’ti aitaina añ‍ñassa visiṭṭhassa abhāvadīpanatau paripuṇṇatāya| paṭhamaina vā pahānasampadaṃ dhammassa dassaiti, dutiyaina pabhāvasampadaṃ|

aivaṃ  saṅkhaipainaiva sabbadhammaguṇaihi saddhammaṃ thaumaitvā idāni ariyasaṅghaṃ thaumaituṃ ‘‘guṇaihī’’tiādimāha| ‘‘guṇaihī’’ti padassa ‘‘yuttau’’ti iminā sambandhau| idāni yaihi guṇaihi yuttau, tai dassaintau ‘‘sīlasamādhī’’tiādimāha| tattha catupārisuddhisīlādi ‘‘sīla’’nti vuc‍cati| samādhīti paṭhamajjhānādi| samādhisīsaina hi paṭhamajjhānādayau vuttā| pañ‍ñāti maggapañ‍ñā| vimutti ca vimuttiñāṇañ‍ca vimuttivimuttiñāṇanti vattabbai aikadaisasarūpaikasaisanayaina ‘‘vimuttiñāṇa’’nti vuttaṃ| ādisaddapariyāyaina pabhutisaddaina vā vimuttiggahaṇaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| tattha vimuttīti phalaṃ| vimuttiñāṇanti pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaṃ| pabhuti-saddaina chaḷabhiñ‍ñācatupaṭisambhidādayau guṇā saṅgahitāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aittha ca sīlādayau guṇā laukiyā laukuttarā ca yathāsambhavaṃ niddiṭṭhāti vaiditabbā| yaṃ panaittha kainaci vuttaṃ ‘‘sīlādayau kiñ‍cāpi laukiyalaukuttarā yathāsambhavaṃ labbhanti, tathāpi antai ‘ariyasaṅgha’nti vacanatau sīlādayau cattārau dhammakkhandhā laukuttarāvā’’ti, taṃ tassa matimattaṃ| na hi ariyasaṅghassa laukiyaguṇaihipi thaumanāya kauci dausau dissati, sabbañ‍ñubuddhassapi tāva laukiyalaukuttaraguṇaihi thaumanā hauti, kimaṅgaṃ pana ariyasaṅghassāti|

kusalatthikānaṃ janānaṃ puñ‍ñassa vuddhiyā khaittasadisattā khaittanti āha ‘‘khaittaṃ janānaṃ kusalatthikāna’’nti| khittaṃ bījaṃ mahapphalabhāvakaraṇaina tāyatīti hi khaittaṃ, pubbaṇṇāparaṇṇaviruhanabhūmi, taṃsadisattā ariyasaṅghaupi ‘‘khaitta’’nti vuc‍cati| iminā ariyasaṅghassa anuttarapuñ‍ñakkhaittabhāvaṃ dīpaiti| ‘‘anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti hi vuttaṃ| tanti pubbai ‘‘yau’’ti aniyamaina vuttassa niyamavacanaṃ| ariyasaṅghanti aittha ārakattā kilaisaihi, anayai na iriyanatau, ayai ca iriyanatau ariyā niruttinayaina| atha vā sadaivakaina laukaina saraṇanti araṇīyatau upagantabbatau upagatānañ‍ca tadatthasiddhitau ariyā| ariyānaṃ saṅghau samūhauti ariyasaṅghau| atha vā ariyau ca sau yathāvuttanayaina saṅghau ca diṭṭhisīlasāmañ‍ñaina saṃhatabhāvatauti ariyasaṅghau, aṭṭha ariyapuggalā| taṃ ariyasaṅghaṃ| bhagavatau aparabhāgai buddhadhammaratanānampi samadhigamau saṅgharatanādhīnauti ariyasaṅghassa bahūpakārataṃ dassaituṃ idhaiva ‘‘sirasā namāmī’’ti vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

aivaṃ  gāthāttayaina saṅkhaipatau sakalaguṇasaṃkittanamukhaina ratanattayassa paṇāmaṃ katvā idāni taṃ nipac‍cakāraṃ yathādhippaitai payaujanai pariṇāmaintau āha ‘‘ic‍caiva’’mic‍cādi| ic‍caivaṃ yathāvuttanayaina ac‍cantaṃ aikantaina namassanaiyyaṃ namassitabbaṃ ratanattayaṃ namassamānau kāyavācācittaihi vandamānau ahaṃ vipulaṃ yaṃ puñ‍ñābhisandaṃ alatthanti sambandhau| tattha buddhādayau  ratijananaṭṭhaina ratanaṃ| taisañhi ‘‘itipi sau bhagavā’’tiādinā yathābhūtaguṇai āvaj‍jaintassa amatādhigamahaitubhūtaṃ anappakaṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍jati| yathāha –

‘‘yasmiṃ, mahānāma, samayai ariyasāvakau tathāgataṃ anussarati, naivassa tasmiṃ samayai rāgapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na dausapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, na mauhapariyuṭṭhitaṃ cittaṃ hauti, ujugatamaivassa tasmiṃ samayai cittaṃ hauti, ujugatacittau khau pana, mahānāma, ariyasāvakau labhati atthavaidaṃ, labhati dhammavaidaṃ, labhati dhammūpasaṃhitaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, pamuditassa pīti jāyatī’’tiādi (a॰ ni॰ 11.11)|

cittīkatādibhāvau vā ratanaṭṭhau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘cittīkataṃ mahagghañ‍ca, atulaṃ dul‍labhadassanaṃ|

anaumasattaparibhaugaṃ, ratanaṃ taina vuc‍catī’’ti|| (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.33)|

cittīkatabhāvādayau ca anañ‍ñasādhāraṇā buddhādīsuyaiva labbhantīti|

‘‘puñ‍ñābhisandanti puñ‍ñarāsiṃ puñ‍ñappavattaṃ vā’’ti mahāgaṇṭhipadai vuttaṃ| majjhimagaṇṭhipadai pana cūḷagaṇṭhipadai ca ‘‘puñ‍ñābhisandanti puñ‍ñābhinisaṃsa’’ntipi atthau vuttau| puñ‍ñābhisandanti puñ‍ñanadiṃ, puñ‍ñappavāhanti aivaṃ panaittha atthau vaiditabbau| avicchaidaina pavattiyamānañhi puñ‍ñaṃ abhisandanaṭṭhaina ‘‘puñ‍ñābhisandau’’ti vuc‍cati| tainaiva sāratthapakāsiniyā saṃyuttanikāyaṭṭhakathāya (saṃ॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 3.5.1027) –

‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandā sukhassāhārā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti ‘itipi  sau bhagavā…pai॰… buddhau bhagavā’ti, ayaṃ paṭhamau puñ‍ñābhisandau kusalābhisandau sukhassāhārau’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1027) –

aivamādikāya pāḷiyā atthaṃ dassaintau ‘‘puñ‍ñābhisandā kusalābhisandāti puñ‍ñanadiyau kusalanadiyau’’ti vuttaṃ| yaṃ pana gaṇṭhipadai vuttaṃ ‘‘puñ‍ñābhisandanti puñ‍ñaphala’’nti, taṃ na sundaraṃ| na hi ratanattayaṃ namassamānau tasmiṃ khaṇai puñ‍ñaphalaṃ alattha, kintu anappakaṃ puñ‍ñarāsiṃ tadā alabhi, tassa ca phalaṃ paralaukabhāgī, diṭṭhadhammai tu antarāyavighātau tassa ca puñ‍ñassa ānisaṃsamattakaṃ, ‘‘tassānubhāvaina hatantarāyau’’ti ca vuttaṃ, na ca puñ‍ñaphalai anuppan‍nai tassānubhāvaina hatantarāyabhāvau na sijjhati, na caitaṃ tasmiṃyaiva khaṇai diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ ahausi| tasmā tassa mahatau puñ‍ñappavāhassa ānubhāvaina hatantarāyauti ayamaiva atthau yuj‍jati| athāpi paṇāmakiriyāya janitattā puñ‍ñamaiva puñ‍ñaphalanti tassādhippāyau siyā, aivaṃ sati yuj‍jaiyya| sau ca puñ‍ñappavāhau na appamattakau, atha khau mahantauyaivāti dassaintau āha ‘‘vipula’’nti, mahantaṃ anappakanti vuttaṃ hauti| alatthanti alabhiṃ, pāpuṇinti atthau|

tassānubhāvaināti tassa yathāvuttassa puñ‍ñappavāhassa ānubhāvaina balaina| hatantarāyauti taṃtaṃsampattiyā vibandhanavasaina sattasantānassa antarai vaimajjhai aiti āgacchatīti antarāyau, diṭṭhadhammikādianatthau| paṇāmapayaujanai vuttavidhinā hatau viddhastau antarāyau upaddavau assāti hatantarāyau| assa ‘‘vaṇṇayissaṃ vinaya’’nti iminā sambandhau, hatantarāyau hutvā vinayaṃ vaṇṇayissanti vuttaṃ hauti| aitaina tassa puñ‍ñappavāhassa attanau pasādasampattiyā ratanattayassa ca khaittabhāvasampattiyā atthasaṃvaṇṇanāya upaghātakaupaddavānaṃ hananai samatthataṃ dīpaiti|

aivaṃ ratanattayassa nipac‍cakārakaraṇai payaujanaṃ dassaitvā idāni yassa vinayapiṭakassa atthaṃ saṃvaṇṇaitukāmau, tassa tāva bhagavatau sāsanassa mūlapatiṭṭhānabhāvaṃ dassaitvā tampi thaumaintau āha ‘‘yasmiṃ ṭhitai’’tiādi| aṭṭhitassa susaṇṭhitassa bhagavatau sāsanaṃ yasmiṃ  ṭhitai patiṭṭhitaṃ hautīti yaujaitabbaṃ| tattha yasminti yasmiṃ vinayapiṭakai| ṭhitaiti pāḷitau ca atthatau ca anūnaṃ hutvā laj‍jīpuggalaisu pavattanaṭṭhaina ṭhitaiti atthau| sāsananti adhisīlaadhicittaadhipañ‍ñāsaṅkhātasikkhattayasaṅgahitaṃ sāsanaṃ| aṭṭhitassāti kāmasukhal‍likattakilamathānuyaugasaṅkhātai antadvayai aṭṭhitassāti atthau| ‘‘appatiṭṭhaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, anāyūhaṃ aughamatari’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.1) hi vuttaṃ| ayañ‍catthau tīsupi sīhaḷagaṇṭhipadaisu vuttauyaiva| gaṇṭhipadai pana ‘‘aṭṭhitassāti parinibbutassapi bhagavatau’’ti vuttaṃ|

patiṭṭhitaṃ hautīti taisuyaiva laj‍jīpuggalaisu pavattanaṭṭhaina patiṭṭhitaṃ hauti| susaṇṭhitassāti aittha tāva tīsupi gaṇṭhipadaisu idaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘dvattiṃsamahāpurisalakkhaṇaasītianubyañ‍janaihi saman‍nāgamanavasaina  susaṇṭhānassāti atthau| anaina assa rūpakāyasampattiṃ nidassaitī’’ti| gaṇṭhipadai pana ‘‘yathāṭhānai patiṭṭhitaihi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatattā rūpakāyaina susaṇṭhitau, kāyavaṅkādirahitattā tādilakkhaṇasaman‍nāgatattā ca nāmakāyainapī’’ti vuttaṃ| kainaci pana ‘‘catubrahmavihāravasaina sattaisu suṭṭhu sammā ca ṭhitassāti atthavasaina vā susaṇṭhitassa| susaṇṭhitattā haisa kaivalaṃ sattānaṃ dukkhaṃ apanaitukāmau hitaṃ upasaṃharitukāmau sampattiyā ca pamuditau apakkhapatitau ca hutvā vinayaṃ daisaiti| tasmā imasmiṃ vinayasaṃvaṇṇanādhikārai sāruppāya thutiyā thaumaintau āha ‘susaṇṭhitassā’’’ti vatvā ‘‘gaṇṭhipadaisu vuttatthau adhippaitādhikārānurūpau na hautī’’ti vuttaṃ| ayaṃ panaittha amhākaṃ khanti – yathāvuttakāmasukhal‍likādiantadvayai aṭṭhitattāyaiva majjhimāya paṭipadāya sammā ṭhitattā susaṇṭhitassāti aivamatthau gahaitabbauti| aivañhi sati ārambhānurūpathaumanā katā hauti yathāvuttaantadvayaṃ vivaj‍jaitvā majjhimāya paṭipadāya vinayapañ‍ñattiyāyaiva yaibhuyyaina pakāsanatau|

tanti pubbai ‘‘yasmi’’nti aniyamaitvā vuttassa niyamavacanaṃ, tassa ‘‘vinaya’’nti iminā sambandhau| asammissanti bhāvanapuṃsakaniddaisau, nikāyantaraladdhīhi asammissaṃ katvā anākulaṃ katvā vaṇṇayissanti vuttaṃ hauti| sikkhāpadapañ‍ñattiyā anurūpassa kālamattassapi dhammasaināpatisāriputtatthairasadisainapi duviñ‍ñaiyyabhāvatau kaivalaṃ buddhavisayaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ attanau  balaina vaṇṇayissāmīti vacanamattampi añ‍ñaihi vattumasak‍kuṇaiyyattā ‘‘nissāya pubbācariyānubhāva’’nti āha| pubbācariyānubhāvau nāma atthatau pubbācariyaihi saṃvaṇṇitā aṭṭhakathā, tatauyaiva ca ‘‘pubbācariyānubhāvau aṭṭhakathā’’ti sabbattha gaṇṭhipadaisu vuttaṃ| tasmā pubbācariyaihi saṃvaṇṇitaṃ aṭṭhakathaṃ nissāya vaṇṇayissaṃ, na attanauyaiva balaṃ nissāyāti vuttaṃ hauti|

atha ‘‘paurāṇaṭṭhakathāsu vij‍jamānāsu puna vinayasaṃvaṇṇanāya kiṃ payaujana’’nti yau vadaiyya, tassa paurāṇaṭṭhakathāya anūnabhāvaṃ attanau ca saṃvaṇṇanāya payaujanaṃ dassaintau ‘‘kāmañ‍cā’’tiādimāha| kāmanti aikantaina, yathicchakaṃ vā, sabbasauti vuttaṃ hauti, tassa ‘‘saṃvaṇṇitau’’ti iminā sambandhau| kāmaṃ saṃvaṇṇitauyaiva, nau na saṃvaṇṇitauti atthau| kaihi pana sau vinayau saṃvaṇṇitauti āha ‘‘pubbācariyāsabhaihī’’ti| mahākassapatthairādayau pubbācariyā aiva akampiyaṭṭhaina uttamaṭṭhaina ca āsabhā, taihi pubbācariyāsabhaihīti vuttaṃ hauti| kīdisā panaitai pubbācariyāti āha ‘‘ñāṇambū’’tiādi| aggamaggañāṇasaṅkhātaina ambunā  salilaina niddhautāni nissaisatau āyatiṃ anuppattidhammatāpādanaina dhautāni vikkhālitāni visaudhitāni rāgādīni tīṇi malāni kāmāsavādayau ca cattārau āsavā yaihi tai ñāṇambuniddhātamalāsavā, taihīti atthau| iminā ca na kaivalaṃ aitaisu ācariyabhāvauyaiva, atha khau rāgādimalarahitā khīṇāsavā visuddhasattā aitaiti dassaiti|

khīṇāsavabhāvaipi na aitai sukkhavipassakā, atha khau aivarūpaihipi ānubhāvaihi saman‍nāgatāti dassaintau āha ‘‘visuddhavij‍jāpaṭisambhidaihī’’ti| visuddhā ac‍cantaparisuddhā vij‍jā catassau ca paṭisambhidā yaisaṃ tai visuddhavij‍jāpaṭisambhidā, taihi| aikadaisaina paṭisambhidaṃ appattānaṃ ariyānamaiva abhāvatau aitaihi adhigatapaṭisambhidā paṭutaraladdhappabhaidāti dassaituṃ visuddhaggahaṇaṃ kataṃ| vij‍jāti tissau vij‍jā, aṭṭha vij‍jā vā| tattha dibbacakkhuñāṇaṃ pubbainivāsañāṇaṃ āsavakkhayañāṇañ‍cāti imā tissau vij‍jā| aṭṭha vij‍jā pana –

‘‘vipassanāñāṇamanaumayiddhi,

iddhippabhaidaupi ca dibbasautaṃ|

parassa caitaupariyāyañāṇaṃ,

pubbainivāsānugatañ‍ca ñāṇaṃ|

dibbañ‍ca cakkhāsavasaṅkhayau ca,

aitāni ñāṇāni idhaṭṭha vij‍jā’’ti|| –

aivaṃ vipassanāñāṇamanaumayiddhīhi saddhiṃ pariggahitā cha abhiñ‍ñāyaiva| atthapaṭisambhidā dhammapaṭisambhidā niruttipaṭisambhidā paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāti catassau paṭisambhidā| tattha saṅkhaipatau haituphalai ñāṇaṃ atthapaṭisambhidā, haitumhi ñāṇaṃ dhammapaṭisambhidā, haituhaituphalānurūpaṃ vauhāraisu ñāṇaṃ niruttipaṭisambhidā, idaṃ ñāṇaṃ imamatthaṃ jautayatīti iminā ākāraina haiṭṭhā vuttaisu tīsu ñāṇaisu pavattañāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| aitāsaṃ pana vitthārakathā atipapañ‍cabhāvatau idha na vuc‍cati| paṭisambhidāppattānaṃ saddhammaisu chaikabhāvatau āha ‘‘saddhammasaṃvaṇṇanakauvidaihī’’ti| ‘‘paṭisambhidāppattānampi dhammaisu abhiyaugavasaina visaisau hautīti laddhapaṭisambhidāsu sātisayataṃ dassaituṃ āhā’’tipi vadanti| saddhammasaṃvaṇṇanakauvidaihīti piṭakattayasaṅkhātassa saddhammassa saṃvaṇṇanai sabbasau atthappakāsanai kauvidaihi chaikaihi, kusalaihīti atthau|

kilaisajālaṃ  parikkhārabāhul‍laṃ vā saṃlikhati tanuṃ karautīti sal‍laikhau| idha pana khīṇāsavādhikārattā parikkhārabāhul‍lassa sal‍likhanavasainaiva atthau gahaitabbau, tatauyaiva ca gaṇṭhipadai ‘‘sal‍laikhiyai parimitaparikkhāravuttiyā’’ti atthau vuttau| sal‍laikhassa bhāvau sal‍laikhiyaṃ, tasmiṃ sal‍laikhiyai, sal‍laikhapaṭipattiyanti vuttaṃ hauti| nausulabhūpamaihīti asulabhūpamaihi sal‍laikhapaṭipattiyā asukasadisāti taisaṃ upamāya anucchavikapuggalānaṃ dul‍labhattā natthi sulabhā upamā aitaisanti nausulabhūpamā| mahāvihārassāti cittalapabbataabhayagirisaisanikāyadvayaṃ paṭikkhipati| dhajūpamaihīti rathassa sañ‍jānanahaitukaṃ rathai baddhadhajaṃ viya ajānantānaṃ ‘‘asukaihi ca asukaihi ca thairaihi nivāsitau  mahāvihārau nāmā’’ti aivaṃ mahāvihārassa sañ‍jānanahaituttā mahāvihārassa dhajūpamaihi| saṃvaṇṇitauti sammā anūnaṃ katvā vaṇṇitau| saṃvaṇṇitau ayaṃ vinayauti padacchaidau kātabbau| cittaihi nayaihīti anaikappabhaidanayattā vicittaihi nayaihi| sambuddhavaranvayaihīti sabbañ‍ñubuddhavaraṃ anugataihi, bhagavatau adhippāyānugataihi nayaihīti vuttaṃ hauti| atha vā buddhavaraṃ anugataihi pubbācariyāsabhaihīti sambandhau kātabbau|

aivaṃ paurāṇaṭṭhakathāya anūnabhāvaṃ dassaitvā idāni attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya payaujanavisaisaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘saṃvaṇṇanā’’tiādimāha| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – kiñ‍cāpi pubbācariyāsabhaihi yathāvuttaguṇavisiṭṭhaihi ayaṃ vinayau sabbasau vaṇṇitau, tathāpi taisaṃ aisā saṃvaṇṇanā sīhaḷadīpavāsīnaṃ bhāsāya saṅkhatattā racitattā dīpantarai bhikkhujanassa sīhaḷadīpatau añ‍ñadīpavāsinau bhikkhugaṇassa kiñ‍ci atthaṃ payaujanaṃ yasmā nābhisambhuṇāti na sampādaiti na sādhaiti, tasmā imaṃ saṃvaṇṇanaṃ pāḷinayānurūpaṃ katvā buddhasiritthairaina ajjhiṭṭhau idāni samārabhissanti| tattha saṃvaṇṇiyati atthau aitāyāti saṃvaṇṇanā, aṭṭhakathā| sā pana dhammasaṅgāhakatthairaihi paṭhamaṃ tīṇi piṭakāni saṅgāyitvā tassa atthavaṇṇanānurūpainaiva vācanāmaggaṃ āraupitattā tissau saṅgītiyau āruḷhauyaiva buddhavacanassa atthasaṃvaṇṇanābhūtau kathāmaggau| sauyaiva ca mahāmahindatthairaina tambapaṇṇidīpaṃ ābhatau, pacchā tambapaṇṇiyaihi mahāthairaihi nikāyantaraladdhīhi saṅkarapariharaṇatthaṃ sīhaḷabhāsāya ṭhapitau| taināha ‘‘sīhaḷadīpakainā’’tiādi| sīhassa lānatau gahaṇatau sīhaḷau, sīhakumārau| taṃvaṃsajātatāya tambapaṇṇidīpai khattiyānaṃ taisaṃ nivāsatāya tambapaṇṇidīpassapi sīhaḷabhāvau vaiditabbau, tasmiṃ sīhaḷadīpai bhūtattā sīhaḷadīpakaina vākyaina vacanaina, sīhaḷabhāsāyāti vuttaṃ hauti|

pāḷinayānurūpanti pāḷinayassa anurūpaṃ katvā, māgadhabhāsāya parivattitvāti vuttaṃ hauti| ajjhaisananti garuṭṭhāniyaṃ payirupāsitvā garutaraṃ payaujanaṃ uddissa abhipatthanā ajjhaisanā, taṃ ajjhaisanaṃ, āyācananti atthau| tassa ‘‘samanussarantau’’ti iminā sambandhau| kassa ajjhaisananti  āha ‘‘buddhasirivhayassa thairassā’’ti| buddhasirīti avhayau nāmaṃ yassa sauyaṃ buddhasirivhayau, tassa, itthan‍nāmassa thairassa ajjhaisanaṃ sammā ādaraina samanussarantau hadayai ṭhapaintauti atthau|

idāni attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya karaṇappakāraṃ dassaintau ‘‘saṃvaṇṇanaṃ tañ‍cā’’tiādimāha| tattha tañ‍ca idāni vuc‍camānaṃ saṃvaṇṇanaṃ samārabhantau sakalāyapi mahāaṭṭhakathāya idha gahaitabbatau mahāaṭṭhakathaṃ tassā idāni vuc‍camānāya saṃvaṇṇanāya sarīraṃ katvā mahāpac‍cariyaṃ yau vinicchayau vuttau, tathaiva kurundīnāmādīsu vissutāsu aṭṭhakathāsu yau vinicchayau vuttau, tataupi vinicchayatau yuttamatthaṃ aparic‍cajantau antaugadhatthairavādaṃ katvā saṃvaṇṇanaṃ samārabhissanti padatthasambandhau vaiditabbau| aittha ca atthau kathiyati aitāyāti atthakathā, sāyaiva aṭṭhakathā tthakārassa ṭṭhakāraṃ katvā ‘‘dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau’’tiādīsu (paṭi॰ ma॰ 1.17; 2.8) viya| mahāpac‍cariyanti aittha pac‍carīti uḷumpaṃ vuc‍cati, tasmiṃ nisīditvā katattā tamaiva nāmaṃ jātaṃ| kurundival‍livihārau nāma atthi, tattha katattā kurundīti nāmaṃ jātanti vadanti| ādisaddaina andhakaṭṭhakathaṃ saṅkhaipaṭṭhakathañ‍ca saṅgaṇhāti| vissutāsūti sabbattha patthaṭāsu, pākaṭāsūti vuttaṃ hauti|

yuttamatthanti aittha tāva majjhimagaṇṭhipadai cūḷagaṇṭhipadai ca idaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘yuttamatthanti saṃvaṇṇaitabbaṭṭhānassa yuttamatthaṃ, na pana tattha ayuttampi atthīti vuttaṃ hautī’’ti| mahāgaṇṭhipadai panaittha na kiñ‍ci vuttaṃ| kainaci pana ‘‘mahāaṭṭhakathānayaina vinayayuttiyā vā yuttamattha’’nti vuttaṃ, taṃ yuttaṃ viya dissati mahāpac‍cariādīsupi katthaci ayuttassāpi atthassa upari vibhāvanatau| ‘‘aṭṭhakathaṃyaiva gahaitvā saṃvaṇṇanaṃ karissāmī’’ti vuttai aṭṭhakathāsu vuttatthairavādānaṃ bāhirabhāvau siyāti taipi antaukattukāmau ‘‘antaugadhathairavāda’’nti āha, thairavādaipi antaukatvāti vuttaṃ hauti| saṃvaṇṇananti aparakālakiriyāya kammaniddaisau| pubbai vuttaṃ tu ‘‘saṃvaṇṇana’’nti vacanaṃ tatthaiva ‘‘samārabhantau’’ti pubbakālakiriyāya kammabhāvaina yaujaitabbaṃ| sammāti vattabbai gāthābandhavasaina rassabhāvau katauti vaiditabbau|

aivaṃ  karaṇappakāraṃ dassaitvā idāni sautūhi paṭipaj‍jitabbavidhiṃ dassaintau ‘‘taṃ mai’’tiādimāha| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – idāni vuc‍camānaṃ taṃ mama saṃvaṇṇanaṃ dhammapadīpassa tathāgatassa  dhammaṃ sāsanadhammaṃ pāḷidhammaṃ vā sak‍kac‍caṃ paṭimānayantā pūjaintā thiraihi sīlakkhandhādīhi saman‍nāgatattā thairā, acirapabbajitattā navā, taisaṃ majjhai bhavattā majjhimā ca bhikkhū pasan‍nacittā yathāvuttanayaina sappayaujanattā upari vakkhamānavidhinā pamāṇattā ca saddahitvā pītisaumanassayuttacittā issāpakatā ahutvā nisāmaintu suṇantūti| tattha dhammappadīpassāti dhammauyaiva sattasantānaisu mauhandhakāravidhamanatau padīpasadisattā padīpau assāti dhammapadīpau, bhagavā| tassa dhammapadīpassa|

idāni attanau saṃvaṇṇanāya āgamavisuddhiṃ dassaitvā pamāṇabhāvaṃ dassaintau ‘‘buddhainā’’tiādimāha| yathaiva buddhaina yau dhammau ca vinayau ca vuttau, sau tassa buddhassa yaihi puttaihi dhammasaināpatiādīhi tathaiva ñātau, taisaṃ buddhaputtānaṃ matimac‍cajantā sīhaḷaṭṭhakathācariyā yasmā purai aṭṭhakathā akaṃsūti ayamaittha sambandhau| tattha dhammauti suttābhidhammai saṅgaṇhāti, vinayauti sakalaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ| aittāvatā ca sabbampi buddhavacanaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ hauti| sakalañhi buddhavacanaṃ dhammavinayavasaina duvidhaṃ hauti| vuttauti pāḷitau ca atthatau ca buddhaina bhagavatā vuttau| na hi bhagavatā abyākataṃ nāma tantipadaṃ atthi, sabbaisaṃyaiva atthau kathitau, tasmā sammāsambuddhainaiva tiṇṇaṃ piṭakānaṃ atthavaṇṇanāk‍kamaupi bhāsitauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| tattha tattha bhagavatā pavattitā pakiṇṇakadaisanāyaiva hi aṭṭhakathā| tathaiva ñātauti yathaiva buddhaina vuttau, tathaiva aikapadampi aikakkharampi avināsaitvā adhippāyañ‍ca avikaupaitvā ñātau viditauti atthau| taisaṃ matimac‍cajantāti taisaṃ buddhaputtānaṃ adhippāyaṃ aparic‍cajantā| aṭṭhakathā akaṃsūti aṭṭhakathāyau akaṃsu| katthaci ‘‘aṭṭhakathāmakaṃsū’’ti pāṭhau dissati, tatthāpi sauyaivatthau, ma-kārau pana padasandhivasaina āgatauti daṭṭhabbau| ‘‘aṭṭhakathā’’ti bahuvacananiddaisaina mahāpac‍cariyādikaṃ saṅgaṇhāti|

tasmāti yasmā taisaṃ buddhaputtānaṃ adhippāyaṃ avikaupaitvā purai aṭṭhakathā akaṃsu, tasmāti atthau| hīti nipātamattaṃ haituatthassa  ‘‘tasmā’’ti imināyaiva pakāsitattā| yadi aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ sabbampi pamāṇaṃ, aivaṃ sati tattha pamādalaikhāpi pamāṇaṃ siyāti āha ‘‘vaj‍jayitvāna pamādalaikha’’nti| tattha pamādalaikhanti aparabhāgai pautthakāruḷhakālai pamaj‍jitvā likhanavasaina pavattaṃ pamādapāṭhaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – pamādaina satiṃ apac‍cupaṭṭhapaitvā adin‍nādānassa pubbapayaugai ‘‘sac‍caipi alikaipi duk‍kaṭa’’nti vuttavacanasadisaṃ yaṃ likhitaṃ, taṃ vaj‍jayitvā apanaitvā sabbaṃ pamāṇanti| vakkhati hi tattha –

‘‘mahāaṭṭhakathāyaṃ  pana sac‍caipi alikaipi duk‍kaṭamaiva vuttaṃ, taṃ pamādalikhitanti vaiditabbaṃ| na hi adin‍nādānassa pubbapayaugai pācittiyaṭṭhānai duk‍kaṭaṃ nāma atthī’’ti (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.94)|

kaisaṃ pamāṇanti āha ‘‘sikkhāsu sagāravānaṃ idha paṇḍitāna’’nti| idhāti imasmiṃ sāsanai| puna ‘‘yasmā’’ti vacanassa kau sambandhauti cai? aittha tāva mahāgaṇṭhipadai gaṇṭhipadai ca na kiñ‍ci vuttaṃ, majjhimagaṇṭhipadai pana cūḷagaṇṭhipadai ca idaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘yasmā pamāṇaṃ, tasmā nisāmaintu pasan‍nacittā’’ti| aivamassa sambandhau daṭṭhabbau| yasmā aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ pamāṇaṃ, tasmā idha vuttampi pamāṇamaivāti pāṭhasaisaṃ katvā vajirabuddhitthairau vadati| tattha idhāti imissā samantapāsādikāyāti atthau gahaitabbau|

tattha ‘‘yasmā’’ti vacanassa paṭhamaṃ vuttasambandhavasaina aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ sabbampi pamāṇanti sādhitattā idāni vuc‍camānāpi saṃvaṇṇanā kaivalaṃ vacanamattainaiva bhin‍nā, atthatau pana aṭṭhakathāyaivāti dassaituṃ ‘‘tatau ca bhāsantaramaivā’’tiādimāha| pacchā vuttasambandhavasaina pana idha vuttampi kasmā pamāṇanti cai? yasmā vacanamattaṃ ṭhapaitvā aisāpi aṭṭhakathāyaiva, tasmā pamāṇanti dassaituṃ ‘‘tatau ca bhāsantaramaivā’’tiādimāha| aivamākulaṃ dubbiñ‍ñaiyyasabhāvañ‍ca katvā gaṇṭhipadaisu sambandhau dassitau, anākulavacanau ca bhadantabuddhaghausācariyau| na hi sau aivamākulaṃ katvā vattumarahati, tasmā yathādhippaitamatthamanākulaṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyañ‍ca katvā yathāṭhitassa sambandhavasainaiva dassayissāma| kathaṃ? yasmā aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ pamāṇaṃ, tasmā sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāti aivamaittha sambandhau daṭṭhabbau| yadi nāma aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ pamāṇaṃ, ayaṃ pana  idāni vuc‍camānā kasmā sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāti āha ‘‘tatau ca bhāsantaramaiva hitvā’’tiādi| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yasmā aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ pamāṇaṃ, yasmā ca ayaṃ saṃvaṇṇanāpi bhāsantaraparic‍cāgādimattavisiṭṭhā, atthatau pana abhin‍nāva, tatauyaiva ca pamāṇabhūtā haissati, tasmā sak‍kac‍caṃ ādaraṃ katvā anusikkhitabbāti| tathā hi paurāṇaṭṭhakathānaṃ pamāṇabhāvau, imissā ca saṃvaṇṇanāya bhāsantaraparic‍cāgādimattavisiṭṭhāya atthatau tatau abhin‍nabhāvauti ubhayampaitaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbabhāvahaitūti daṭṭhabbaṃ| na hi kaivalaṃ paurāṇaṭṭhakathānaṃ satipi pamāṇabhāvai ayaṃ saṃvaṇṇanā tatau bhin‍nā atthatau añ‍ñāyaiva ca sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāti vattumarahati, nāpi imissā saṃvaṇṇanāya tatauabhin‍nabhāvaipi paurāṇaṭṭhakathānaṃ asati pamāṇabhāvai ayaṃ saṃvaṇṇanā sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāti  vattuṃ yuttarūpā hauti, tasmā yathāvuttanayaina ubhayampaitaṃ sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbabhāvahaitūti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

tatauti aṭṭhakathātau| bhāsantaramaiva hitvāti kañ‍cukasadisaṃ sīhaḷabhāsaṃ apanaitvā| vitthāramaggañ‍ca samāsayitvāti paurāṇaṭṭhakathāsu upari vuc‍camānampi ānaitvā tattha tattha papañ‍citaṃ ‘‘ñatticatutthaina kammaina akuppaina ṭhānārahaina upasampan‍nauti bhikkhū’’ti (pārā॰ 45) aittha apalaukanādīnaṃ catun‍nampi kammānaṃ vitthārakathā viya tādisaṃ vitthāramaggaṃ saṅkhipitvā vaṇṇayissāmāti adhippāyau| tathā hi vakkhati –

‘‘aittha ca ñatticatutthakammaṃ aikamaiva āgataṃ, imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai ṭhatvā cattāri saṅghakammāni nīharitvā vitthāratau kathaitabbānīti sabbaaṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ, tāni ca ‘apalaukanakammaṃ ñattikammaṃ ñattidutiyakammaṃ ñatticatutthakamma’nti paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapaitvā vitthāraina khandhakatau parivārāvasānai kammavibhaṅgatau ca pāḷiṃ āharitvā kathitāni| tāni mayaṃ parivārāvasānai kammavibhaṅgaiyaiva vaṇṇayissāma| aivañhi sati paṭhamapārājikavaṇṇanā ca na bhāriyā bhavissati, yathāṭhitāya ca pāḷiyā vaṇṇanā suviñ‍ñaiyyā bhavissati, tāni ca ṭhānāni asuñ‍ñāni bhavissanti, tasmā anupadavaṇṇanamaiva karaumā’’ti (pārā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.45 bhikkhupadabhājanīyavaṇṇanā)|

vinicchayaṃ  sabbamasaisayitvāti taṃtaṃaṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ sabbampi vinicchayaṃ asaisayitvā saisaṃ akatvā, kiñ‍cimattampi aparic‍cajitvāti vuttaṃ hauti| vaṇṇituṃ yuttarūpaṃ hutvā anuk‍kamaina āgataṃ pāḷiṃ aparic‍cajitvā saṃvaṇṇanatau sīhaḷaṭṭhakathāsu ayuttaṭṭhānai vaṇṇitaṃ yathāṭhānaiyaiva saṃvaṇṇanatau ca vuttaṃ ‘‘tantik‍kamaṃ kiñ‍ci avauk‍kamitvā’’ti, kiñ‍ci pāḷik‍kamaṃ anatik‍kamitvā anuk‍kamainaiva vaṇṇayissāmāti adhippāyau|

suttantikānaṃ vacanānamatthanti suttantapāḷiyaṃ āgatānampi vacanānamatthaṃ| sīhaḷaṭṭhakathāsu ‘‘suttantikānaṃ bhārau’’ti vatvā avuttānampi vairañ‍jakaṇḍādīsu jhānakathāānāpānassatisamaādhiādīnaṃ suttantavacanānamatthaṃ taṃtaṃsuttānurūpaṃ sabbasau paridīpayissāmīti adhippāyau| haissatīti bhavissati, kariyissatīti vā atthau| aittha ca paṭhamasmiṃ atthavikappai bhāsantaraparic‍cāgādikaṃ catubbidhaṃ kic‍caṃ nipphādaitvā suttantikānaṃ vacanānamatthaṃ paridīpayantī ayaṃ vaṇṇanā bhavissatīti vaṇṇanāya vasaina samānakattukatā vaiditabbā| pacchimasmiṃ atthavikappai  pana haiṭṭhāvuttabhāsantaraparic‍cāgādiṃ katvā suttantikānaṃ vacanānamatthaṃ paridīpayantī ayaṃ vaṇṇanā amhaihi kariyissatīti aivaṃ ācariyavasaina samānakattukatā vaiditabbā| vaṇṇanāpīti aittha apisaddaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘tasmāpi sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāti yaujaitabba’’nti cūḷagaṇṭhipadai vuttaṃ| tattha pubbai vuttappayaujanavisaisaṃ pamāṇabhāvañ‍ca sampiṇḍaitīti adhippāyau| majjhimagaṇṭhipadai pana ‘‘tasmā sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāpī’’ti sambandhau vuttau| aittha pana na kaivalaṃ ayaṃ vaṇṇanā haissati, atha khau anusikkhitabbāpīti imamatthaṃ sampiṇḍaitīti adhippāyau| aitthāpi yathāṭhitavasainaiva apisaddassa atthau gahaitabbauti amhākaṃ khanti| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yasmā aṭṭhakathāsu vuttaṃ pamāṇaṃ, yasmā ca ayaṃ vaṇṇanāpi tatau abhin‍nattā pamāṇabhūtāyaiva haissati, tasmā sak‍kac‍caṃ anusikkhitabbāti|

ganthārambhakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

bāhiranidānakathā

idāni  ‘‘taṃ vaṇṇayissaṃ vinaya’’nti paṭiñ‍ñātattā yathāpaṭiñ‍ñātavinayasaṃvaṇṇanaṃ kattukāmau saṃvaravinayapahānavinayādivasaina vinayassa bahuvidhattā idha saṃvaṇṇaitabbabhāvaina adhippaitau tāva vinayau vavatthapaitabbauti dassaintau āha ‘‘tatthā’’tiādi| tattha tatthāti tāsu gāthāsu| tāva-saddau paṭhamanti imasmiṃ atthai daṭṭhabbau| taina paṭhamaṃ vinayaṃ vavatthapaitvā pacchā tassa vaṇṇanaṃ karissāmīti dīpaiti| vavatthapaitabbauti niyamaitabbau| tainaitaṃ vuc‍catīti yasmā vavatthapaitabbau, taina haitunā aitaṃ ‘‘vinayau nāmā’’tiādikaṃ niyāmakavacanaṃ vuc‍catīti atthau| assāti vinayassa| mātikāti uddaisau| sau hi niddaisapadānaṃ jananīṭhānai ṭhitattā mātā viyāti mātikāti vuc‍cati|

idāni vaṇṇaitabbamatthaṃ mātikaṃ ṭhapaitvā dassaintau āha ‘‘vuttaṃ yainā’’tiādi| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – aitaṃ ‘‘taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ viharatī’’tiādinidānavacanapaṭimaṇḍitaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃ yaina puggalaina vuttaṃ, yasmiṃ kālai vuttaṃ, yasmā kāraṇā vuttaṃ, yaina dhāritaṃ, yaina ca ābhataṃ, yaisu patiṭṭhitaṃ, aitaṃ yathāvuttavidhānaṃ vatvā tatau ‘‘taina samayainā’’tiādipāṭhassa atthaṃ anaikappakāratau dassayantau vinayassa atthavaṇṇanaṃ karissāmīti| aittha ca ‘‘vuttaṃ yaina yadā yasmā’’ti idaṃ vacanaṃ ‘‘taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā’’tiādinidānavacanamattaṃ apaikkhitvā vattukāmaupi visuṃ avatvā ‘‘nidānaina ādikalyāṇaṃ, ‘idamavaucā’ti nigamanaina pariyausānakalyāṇa’’nti ca vacanatau nidānanigamanānipi satthudaisanāya anuvidhānattā tadantaugadhānaivāti nidānassapi vinayapāḷiyaṃyaiva antaugadhattā ‘‘vuttaṃ yaina yadā yasmā’’ti idampi vinayapiṭakasambandhaṃyaiva katvā mātikaṃ ṭhapaisi| mātikāya hi ‘‘aita’’nti vuttaṃ vinayapiṭakaṃyaiva sāmañ‍ñatau sabbattha sambandhamupagacchati|

idāni pana taṃ visuṃ nīharitvā dassaintau ‘‘tattha vuttaṃ yainā’’tiādimāha| tattha tatthāti taisu mātikāpadaisu| atha kasmā idamaiva vacanaṃ sandhāya vuttanti āha ‘‘idañhī’’tiādi| idanti ‘‘taina samayaina buddhau bhagavā’’tiādivacanaṃ| hi-saddau yasmāti atthai daṭṭhabbau, yasmā buddhassa bhagavatau attapac‍cakkhavacanaṃ na hauti, tasmāti vuttaṃ hauti| attapac‍cakkhavacanaṃ na hautīti attanau pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā vuttavacanaṃ na hauti, bhagavatā vuttavacanaṃ  na hautīti adhippāyau| ‘‘attapac‍cakkhavacanaṃ na hautīti āhac‍ca bhāsitaṃ na hautīti adhippāyau’’ti kainaci vuttaṃ| gaṇṭhipadai pana ‘‘attapac‍cakkhavacanaṃ na hautīti attanau dharamānakālai vuttavacanaṃ na hautī’’ti likhitaṃ| tadubhayampi atthatau samānamaiva| idāni pañhakaraṇaṃ vatvā anuk‍kamaina yathāvuttapañhavissaj‍janaṃ karauntau ‘‘āyasmatā’’tiādimāha| iminā puggalaṃ niyamaiti, ‘‘tañ‍cā’’tiādinā kālaṃ niyamaiti| tañ‍ca upālitthairaina vuttavacanaṃ kālatau paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai vuttanti atthau|

paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikathāvaṇṇanā

idāni taṃ paṭhamamahāsaṅgītiṃ dassaitukāmau tassā tantiāruḷhāya idha vacanai kāraṇaṃ dassaintau ‘‘paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti nāma caisā…pai॰… vaiditabbā’’ti āha| paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti nāma caisāti ca-saddau īdisaisu ṭhānaisu vattabbasampiṇḍanatthau, tañ‍ca paṭhamamahāsaṅgītikālai vuttaṃ, aisā ca paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti aivaṃ vaiditabbāti vuttaṃ hauti| upañ‍ñāsatthau vā ca-saddau| upañ‍ñāsauti ca vākyārambhau vuc‍cati| aisā hi ganthakārānaṃ pakati, yadidaṃ kiñ‍ci vatvā puna paraṃ vattumārabhantānaṃ casaddappayaugau| yaṃ pana kainaci vuttaṃ ‘‘paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti nāma cāti aittha ca-saddau atiraikatthau, taina añ‍ñāpi atthīti dīpaitī’’ti| tadaiva tassa ganthak‍kamai akauvidataṃ dassaiti| na haittha casaddaina atiraikatthau viñ‍ñāyati| yadi caittha aitadatthauyaiva ca-kārau adhippaitau siyā, aivaṃ sati na kattabbauyaiva paṭhamasaddainaiva añ‍ñāsaṃ dutiyādisaṅgītīnampi atthibhāvassa dīpitattā| dutiyādiṃ upādāya hi paṭhamasaddappayaugau dīghādiṃ upādāya rassādisaddappayaugau viya| yathāpac‍cayaṃ tattha tattha daisitattā pañ‍ñattattā ca vippakiṇṇānaṃ dhammavinayānaṃ saṅgahaitvā gāyanaṃ kathanaṃ saṅgīti| aitaina taṃtaṃsikkhāpadānaṃ suttānañ‍ca ādipariyausānaisu antarantarā ca sambandhavasaina ṭhapitaṃ saṅgītikāravacanaṃ  saṅgahitaṃ hauti| mahāvisayattā pūjanīyattā ca mahatī saṅgīti mahāsaṅgīti, paṭhamā mahāsaṅgīti paṭhamamahāsaṅgīti| nidānakausal‍latthanti nidadāti daisanaṃ daisakālādivasaina aviditaṃ viditaṃ katvā nidassaitīti nidānaṃ, tattha kausal‍laṃ nidānakausal‍laṃ, tadatthanti atthau|

sattānaṃ dassanānuttariyasaraṇādipaṭilābhahaitubhūtāsu vij‍jamānāsupi añ‍ñāsu bhagavatau kiriyāsu ‘‘buddhau baudhaiyya’’nti paṭiñ‍ñāya anulaumanatau vainaiyyānaṃ maggaphaluppattihaitubhūtā kiriyā  nippariyāyaina buddhakic‍canti āha ‘‘dhammacak‍kappavattanañhi ādiṃ katvā’’ti| tattha saddhindriyādidhammauyaiva pavattanaṭṭhaina cak‍kanti dhammacak‍kaṃ| atha vā cak‍kanti āṇā, dhammatau anapaitattā dhammañ‍ca taṃ cak‍kañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammaina ñāyaina cak‍kantipi dhammacak‍kaṃ| yathāha –

‘‘dhammañ‍ca pavattaiti cak‍kañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, cak‍kañ‍ca pavattaiti dhammañ‍cāti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammaina pavattaitīti dhammacak‍kaṃ, dhammacariyāya pavattaitīti dhammacak‍ka’’ntiādi (paṭi॰ ma॰ 2.40)|

katabuddhakic‍caiti kataṃ pariniṭṭhāpitaṃ buddhakic‍caṃ yaina, tasmiṃ katabuddhakic‍cai bhagavati laukanāthaiti sambandhau| aitaina buddhakattabbassa kassacipi asaisitabhāvaṃ dassaiti| tatauyaiva hi sau bhagavā parinibbutauti| nanu ca sāvakaihi vinītāpi vinaiyyā bhagavatāyaiva vinītā haunti, tathā hi sāvakabhāsitaṃ suttaṃ buddhavacananti vuc‍cati, sāvakavinaiyyā ca na tāva vinītāti? nāyaṃ dausau taisaṃ vinayanūpāyassa sāvakaisu ṭhapitattā| tainaivāha –

‘‘na tāvāhaṃ pāpima parinibbāyissāmi, yāva na bhikkhū viyattā vinītā visāradā bahussutā āgatāgamā dhammadharā vinayadharā mātikādharā uppan‍naṃ parappavādaṃ sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggahaitvā sappāṭihāriyaṃ dhammaṃ daisaissantī’’tiādi (dī॰ ni॰ 2.168)|

‘‘kusinārāya’’ntiādinā bhagavatau parinibbutadaisakālavisaisadassanaṃ, ‘‘aparinibbutau bhagavā’’ti gāhassa micchābhāvadassanatthaṃ laukai  jātasaṃvaḍḍhabhāvadassanatthañ‍ca| tathā hi manussabhāvassa supākaṭakaraṇatthaṃ mahābaudhisattā carimabhavai dārapariggahādīnipi karauntīti| kusinārāyanti aivaṃnāmakai nagarai| samīpatthai caitaṃ bhummavacanaṃ| upavattanai mal‍lānaṃ sālavanaiti tassa nagarassa upavattanabhūtaṃ mal‍larājūnaṃ sālavanuyyānaṃ dassaiti| tattha nagaraṃ pavisitukāmā uyyānatau upaic‍ca vattanti gacchanti aitaināti upavattananti sālavanaṃ vuc‍cati| yathā hi anurādhapurassa thūpārāmau dakkhiṇapacchimadisāyaṃ, aivaṃ taṃ uyyānaṃ kusinārāya dakkhiṇapacchimadisāya hauti| yathā ca thūpārāmatau dakkhiṇadvāraina nagaraṃ pavisanamaggau pācīnamukhau gantvā uttaraina nivattati, aivaṃ uyyānatau sālapanti pācīnamukhā gantvā uttaraina nivattā, tasmā taṃ ‘‘upavattana’’nti vuc‍cati| yamakasālānamantaraiti yamakasālānaṃ vaimajjhai| tattha  kira bhagavatau pañ‍ñattassa parinibbānamañ‍cassa aikā sālapanti sīsabhāgai hauti, aikā pādabhāgai, tatrāpi aikau taruṇasālau sīsabhāgassa āsan‍nau hauti, aikau pādabhāgassa, tasmā ‘‘yamakasālānamantarai’’ti vuttaṃ| api ca ‘‘yamakasālā nāma mūlakkhandhaviṭapapattaihi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ saṃsibbaitvā ṭhitasālā’’tipi mahāaṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaṃ|

anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyāti upādīyatai kammakilaisaihīti upādi, vipākakkhandhā kaṭattā ca rūpaṃ| sau pana upādi kilaisābhisaṅkhāramāranimmathanaina nibbānappattiyaṃ anaussaṭṭhau, idha khandhamac‍cumāranimmathanaina aussaṭṭhau nisaisitauti ayaṃ anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu natthi aitissā upādisaisauti katvā| nibbānadhātūti caittha nibbutimattaṃ adhippaitaṃ, itthambhūtalakkhaṇai cāyaṃ karaṇaniddaisau| parinibbānaiti parinibbānaṭṭhānai, nimittatthai vā bhummavacanaṃ, parinibbānahaitu san‍nipatitānanti atthau| saṅghassa thairau saṅghatthairau| sau pana saṅghau kiṃparimāṇauti āha ‘‘sattan‍naṃ bhikkhusatasahassāna’’nti| nic‍casāpaikkhattā hi īdisaisu samāsau hautiyaiva yathā ‘‘daivadattassa garukula’’nti| sattan‍naṃ bhikkhusatasahassānanti ca saṅghatthairānaṃyaiva sattan‍naṃ bhikkhusatasahassānaṃ| tadā hi ‘‘san‍nipatitā bhikkhū aittakā’’ti pamāṇarahitā| tathā hi vaiḷuvagāmai vaidanāvikkhambhanatau paṭṭhāya ‘‘na ciraina bhagavā parinibbāyissatī’’ti sutvā tatau tatau āgataisu bhikkhūsu aikabhikkhupi pak‍kantau nāma natthi, tasmā gaṇanaṃ vītivattau saṅghau ahausi| āyasmā  mahākassapau dhammavinayasaṅgāyanatthaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisīti sambandhau|

tattha mahākassapauti mahantaihi sīlakkhandhādīhi saman‍nāgatattā mahantau kassapauti mahākassapau, apica kumārakassapatthairaṃ upādāya ayaṃ mahāthairau ‘‘mahākassapau’’ti vuc‍cati| atha kimatthaṃ āyasmā mahākassapau dhammavinayasaṅgāyanatthaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisīti āha ‘‘sattāhaparinibbutai’’tiādi| satta ahāni samāhaṭāni sattāhaṃ, sattāhaṃ parinibbutassa assāti sattāhaparinibbutau, bhagavā, tasmiṃ sattāhaparinibbutai bhagavati, bhagavatau parinibbānadivasatau paṭṭhāya sattāhai vītivattaiti vuttaṃ hauti| subhaddaina vuḍḍhapabbajitaina vuttavacanaṃ samanussarantauti sambandhau| tattha subhaddauti tassa nāmaṃ, vuḍḍhakālai pana pabbajitattā vuḍḍhapabbajitauti vuc‍cati| ‘‘alaṃ āvusau’’tiādinā taina vuttavacanaṃ nidassaiti| sau hi sattāhaparinibbutai bhagavati āyasmatā mahākassapatthairaina saddhiṃ pāvāya kusināraṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍naisu pañ‍camattaisu bhikkhusataisu avītarāgai bhikkhū antarāmaggai diṭṭhaājīvakassa  santikā bhagavatau parinibbānaṃ sutvā pattacīvarāni chaḍḍaitvā bāhā paggayha nānappakāraṃ paridaivantai disvā aivamāha|

kasmā pana sau aivamāha? bhagavati āghātaina| ayaṃ kira sau khandhakai (mahāva॰ 303) āgatai ātumāvatthusmiṃ nahāpitapubbakau vuḍḍhapabbajitau bhagavati kusināratau nikkhamitvā aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi saddhiṃ ātumaṃ gacchantai ‘‘bhagavā āgacchatī’’ti sutvā āgatakālai ‘‘yāgudānaṃ karissāmī’’ti sāmaṇairabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitai dvai puttai aitadavauca ‘‘bhagavā kira tātā ātumaṃ āgacchati mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ aḍḍhataiḷasaihi bhikkhusataihi, gacchatha tumhai tātā khurabhaṇḍaṃ ādāya nāḷiyāvāpakaina anugharakaṃ anugharakaṃ āhiṇḍatha, lauṇampi tailampi taṇḍulampi khādanīyampi saṃharatha, bhagavatau āgatassa yāgudānaṃ karissāmī’’ti| tai tathā akaṃsu| atha bhagavati ātumaṃ āgantvā bhusāgārakaṃ paviṭṭhai subhaddau sāyanhasamayaṃ gāmadvāraṃ gantvā manussai āmantaitvā ‘‘hatthakammamattaṃ mai daithā’’ti hatthakammaṃ yācitvā ‘‘kiṃ bhantai  karaumā’’ti vuttai ‘‘idañ‍cidañ‍ca gaṇhathā’’ti sabbūpakaraṇāni gāhāpaitvā vihārai uddhanāni kāraitvā aikaṃ kāḷakaṃ kāsāvaṃ nivāsaitvā tādisamaiva pārupitvā ‘‘idaṃ karautha, idaṃ karauthā’’ti sabbarattiṃ vicāraintau satasahassaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā bhauj‍jayāguñ‍ca madhugauḷakañ‍ca paṭiyādāpaisi| bhauj‍jayāgu nāma bhuñ‍jitvā pātabbayāgu, tattha sappimadhuphāṇitamacchamaṃsapupphaphalarasādi yaṃ kiñ‍ci khādanīyaṃ nāma atthi, taṃ sabbaṃ pavisati, kīḷitukāmānaṃ sīsamakkhanayauggā hauti sugandhagandhā|

atha bhagavā kālassaiva sarīrapaṭijagganaṃ katvā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau piṇḍāya carituṃ ātumābhimukhau pāyāsi| manussā tassa āraucaisuṃ ‘‘bhagavā piṇḍāya gāmaṃ pavisati, tayā kassa yāgu paṭiyāditā’’ti| sau yathānivatthapārutaihaiva taihi kāḷakakāsāvaihi aikaina hatthaina dabbiñ‍ca kaṭacchuñ‍ca gahaitvā brahmā viya dakkhiṇajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ bhūmiyaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā vanditvā ‘‘paṭiggaṇhatu mai bhantai bhagavā yāgu’’nti āha| tatau ‘‘jānantāpi tathāgatā pucchantī’’ti khandhakai (mahāva॰ 303) āgatanayaina bhagavā pucchitvā ca sutvā ca taṃ vuḍḍhapabbajitaṃ vigarahitvā tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ akappiyasamādāpanasikkhāpadaṃ khurabhaṇḍapariharaṇasikkhāpadañ‍cāti dvai sikkhāpadāni pañ‍ñapaitvā ‘‘bhikkhavai anaikakappakauṭiyau bhaujanaṃ pariyaisantaihaiva vītināmitā, idaṃ pana tumhākaṃ akappiyaṃ, adhammaina uppan‍nabhaujanaṃ imaṃ paribhuñ‍jitvā anaikāni attabhāvasahassāni apāyaisvaiva nibbattissanti, apaitha mā gaṇhathā’’ti bhikkhācārābhimukhau agamāsi, aikabhikkhunāpi na kiñ‍ci gahitaṃ|

subhaddau  anattamanau hutvā ‘‘ayaṃ ‘sabbaṃ jānāmī’ti āhiṇḍati, sacai na gahaitukāmau paisaitvā āraucaitabbaṃ assa, pak‍kāhārau nāma sabbaciraṃ tiṭṭhantau sattāhamattaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, idañ‍ca mama yāvajīvaṃ pariyattaṃ assa, sabbaṃ taina nāsitaṃ, ahitakāmau ayaṃ mayha’’nti bhagavati āghātaṃ bandhitvā dasabalai dharamānai kiñ‍ci vattuṃ nāsakkhi| aivaṃ kirassa ahausi ‘‘ayaṃ uc‍cakulā pabbajitau mahāpurisau, sacai kiñ‍ci vakkhāmi, mamaṃyaiva santaj‍jaissatī’’ti| svāyaṃ aj‍ja mahākassapatthairaina saddhiṃ āgacchantau ‘‘parinibbutau bhagavā’’ti sutvā laddhassāsau  viya haṭṭhatuṭṭhau aivamāha| thairau pana taṃ sutvā hadayai pahāraṃ viya matthakai patitasuk‍kāsaniṃ viya mañ‍ñi, dhammasaṃvaigau cassa uppaj‍ji ‘‘sattāhamattaparinibbutau bhagavā, aj‍jāpissa suvaṇṇavaṇṇaṃ sarīraṃ dharatiyaiva, dukkhaina bhagavatā ārādhitasāsanai nāma aivaṃ lahuṃ mahantaṃ pāpakasaṭaṃ kaṇṭakau uppan‍nau, alaṃ khau panaisa pāpau vaḍḍhamānau añ‍ñaipi aivarūpai sahāyai labhitvā sāsanaṃ ausak‍kāpaitu’’nti|

tatau thairau cintaisi ‘‘sacai khau panāhaṃ imaṃ mahal‍lakaṃ idhaiva pilautikaṃ nivāsaitvā chārikāya aukirāpaitvā nīharāpaissāmi, manussā ‘samaṇassa gautamassa sarīrai dharamānaiyaiva sāvakā vivadantī’ti amhākaṃ dausaṃ dassaissanti, adhivāsaimi tāva| bhagavatā hi daisitadhammau asaṅgahitapuppharāsisadisau, tattha yathā vātaina pahaṭapupphāni yatau vā tatau vā gacchanti, aivamaiva aivarūpānaṃ vasaina gacchantai gacchantai kālai vinayai aikaṃ dvai sikkhāpadāni nassissanti vinassissanti, suttai aikau dvai pañhavārā nassissanti, abhidhammai aikaṃ dvai bhūmantarāni nassissanti, aivaṃ anuk‍kamaina mūlai naṭṭhai pisācasadisā bhavissāma, tasmā dhammavinayasaṅgahaṃ karissāmi, aivaṃ sati daḷhasuttaina saṅgahitapupphāni viya ayaṃ dhammavinayau nic‍calau bhavissati| aitadatthañhi bhagavā mayhaṃ tīṇi gāvutāni pac‍cuggamanaṃ akāsi, tīhi auvādaihi upasampadaṃ akāsi, kāyatau cīvaraparivattanaṃ akāsi, ākāsai pāṇiṃ cālaitvā candaupamapaṭipadaṃ kathaintau mañ‍ñaiva sakkhiṃ katvā kathaisi, tikkhattuṃ sakalasāsanaratanaṃ paṭicchāpaisi, mādisai bhikkhumhi tiṭṭhamānai ayaṃ pāpau sāsanai vaḍḍhiṃ mā alatthu, yāva adhammau na dippati, dhammau na paṭibāhīyati, avinayau na dippati, vinayau na paṭibāhīyati, adhammavādinau na balavantau haunti, dhammavādinau na dubbalā haunti, avinayavādinau na balavantau haunti, vinayavādinau na dubbalā haunti, tāva dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyissāmi, tatau bhikkhū attanau attanau pahaunakaṃ gahaitvā kappiyākappiyai kathaissanti, athāyaṃ pāpau sayamaiva niggahaṃ pāpuṇissati, puna sīsaṃ ukkhipituṃ na sakkhissati, sāsanaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca bhavissatī’’ti| cintaitvā sau ‘‘aivaṃ nāma mayhaṃ cittaṃ  uppan‍na’’nti kassaci anāraucaitvā  bhikkhusaṅghaṃ samassāsaitvā atha pacchā dhātubhājanadivasai dhammavinayasaṅgāyanatthaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisi| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘āyasmā mahākassapau sattāhaparinibbutai…pai॰… dhammavinayasaṅgāyanatthaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisī’’ti|

tattha alanti paṭikkhaipavacanaṃ| āvusauti paridaivantai bhikkhū ālapati| mā saucitthāti cittai uppan‍nabalavasaukaina mā saucittha| mā paridaivitthāti vācāya mā paridaivittha ‘‘paridaivanaṃ vilāpau’’ti vacanatau| idāni asaucanādīsu kāraṇaṃ dassaintau ‘‘sumuttā maya’’ntiādimāha| taina mahāsamaṇaināti nissak‍kai karaṇavacanaṃ, tatau mahāsamaṇatau suṭṭhu muttā mayanti atthau, upaddutā ca hauma tadāti adhippāyau| haumāti vā atītatthai vattamānavacanaṃ, ahumhāti atthau, anussarantau dhammasaṃvaigavasaināti adhippāyau| dhammasabhāvacintāvasaina pavattaṃ sahauttappañāṇaṃ dhammasaṃvaigau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘sabbasaṅkhatadhammaisu, auttappākārasaṇṭhitaṃ|

ñāṇamauhitabhārānaṃ, dhammasaṃvaigasañ‍ñita’’nti||

ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jatīti tiṭṭhati aittha phalaṃ tadāyattavuttitāyāti ṭhānaṃ, haitu| khauti avadhāraṇai, aitaṃ kāraṇaṃ vij‍jataiva, nau na vij‍jatīti atthau| kiṃ taṃ kāraṇanti āha ‘‘yaṃ pāpabhikkhū’’tiādi| aittha yanti nipātamattaṃ, kāraṇaniddaisau vā, yaina kāraṇaina antaradhāpaiyyuṃ, tadaitaṃ kāraṇaṃ vij‍jatīti atthau| pāpabhikkhūti pāpikāya lāmikāya icchāya saman‍nāgatā bhikkhū| atītau atik‍kantau satthā aittha, aitassāti vā atītasatthukaṃ, pāvacanaṃ| padhānaṃ vacanaṃ pāvacanaṃ, dhammavinayanti vuttaṃ hauti| pakkhaṃ labhitvāti alaj‍jīpakkhaṃ labhitvā| na cirassaivāti na cirainaiva| yāva ca dhammavinayau tiṭṭhatīti yattakaṃ kālaṃ dhammau ca vinayau ca laj‍jīpuggalaisu tiṭṭhati|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatāti parinibbānamañ‍cakai nipan‍naina bhagavatā bhikkhū auvadantaina aitaṃ vuttanti atthau| daisitau pañ‍ñattauti dhammaupi daisitau caiva pañ‍ñattau ca| suttābhidhammasaṅgahitassa hi dhammassa abhisaj‍janaṃ pabaudhanaṃ daisanā, tassaiva pakāratau ñāpanaṃ vinaiyyasantānai ṭhapanaṃ pañ‍ñāpanaṃ, tasmā dhammaupi daisitau caiva pañ‍ñattau cāti  vuttau| pañ‍ñattauti ca ṭhapitauti atthau| vinayaupi daisitau caiva pañ‍ñattau ca| vinayatantisaṅgahitassa hi atthassa kāyavācānaṃ vinayanatau  vinayauti laddhādhivacanassa atisaj‍janaṃ pabaudhanaṃ daisanā, tassaiva pakāratau ñāpanaṃ asaṅkaratau ṭhapanaṃ pañ‍ñāpanaṃ, tasmā vinayaupi daisitau caiva pañ‍ñattau cāti vuc‍cati|

sau vau mamac‍cayaināti sau dhammavinayau tumhākaṃ mamac‍cayaina satthā| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – mayā vau ṭhitainaiva ‘‘idaṃ lahukaṃ, idaṃ garukaṃ, idaṃ sataikicchaṃ, idaṃ ataikicchaṃ, idaṃ laukavaj‍jaṃ, idaṃ paṇṇattivaj‍jaṃ| ayaṃ āpatti puggalassa santikai vuṭṭhāti, ayaṃ gaṇassa, ayaṃ saṅghassa santikai vuṭṭhātī’’ti sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ avītik‍kamanīyatāvasaina autiṇṇavatthusmiṃ sakhandhakaparivārau ubhatauvibhaṅgau mahāvinayau nāma daisitau, taṃ sakalampi vinayapiṭakaṃ mayi parinibbutai tumhākaṃ satthukic‍caṃ sādhaissati ‘‘idaṃ vau kattabbaṃ, idaṃ vau na kattabba’’nti kattabbākattabbassa vibhāgaina anusāsanatau| ṭhitainaiva ca mayā ‘‘imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgāni, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti taina taina vinaiyyānaṃ ajjhāsayānurūpaina pakāraina imai sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhiyadhammai vibhajitvā suttantapiṭakaṃ daisitaṃ, taṃ sakalampi suttantapiṭakaṃ mayi parinibbutai tumhākaṃ satthukic‍caṃ sādhaissati taṃtaṃcariyānurūpaṃ sammāpaṭipattiyā anusāsanatau| ṭhitainaiva ca mayā ‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhā, dvādasāyatanāni, aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau, cattāri sac‍cāni, bāvīsatindriyāni, nava haitū, cattārau āhārā, satta phassā, satta vaidanā, satta sañ‍ñā, satta caitanā, satta cittāni, tatrāpi aittakā dhammā kāmāvacarā, aittakā rūpāvacarā, aittakā arūpāvacarā, aittakā pariyāpan‍nā, aittakā apariyāpan‍nā, aittakā laukiyā, aittakā laukuttarā’’ti imai dhammai vibhajitvā abhidhammapiṭakaṃ daisitaṃ, taṃ sakalampi abhidhammapiṭakaṃ mayi parinibbutai tumhākaṃ satthukic‍caṃ sādhaissati, khandhādivibhāgaina ñāyamānaṃ catusac‍casambaudhāvahattā satthārā sammāsambuddhaina kattabbakic‍caṃ nipphādaissati| iti sabbampaitaṃ abhisambaudhitau yāva parinibbānā pañ‍cacattālīsa vassāni bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ, tīṇi piṭakāni, pañ‍ca nikāyā, navaṅgāni, caturāsīti dhammakkhandhasahassānīti aivaṃ mahappabhaidaṃ hauti| iti imāni caturāsīti  dhammakkhandhasahassāni tiṭṭhanti, ahaṃ aikauva parinibbāyāmi, ahañ‍ca panidāni aikauva auvadāmi anusāsāmi, mayi parinibbutai imāni caturāsīti buddhasahassāni tumhai auvadissanti anusāsissanti auvādānusāsanīkic‍cassa nipphādanatauti|

sāsananti pariyattipaṭipattipaṭivaidhavasaina tividhaṃ sāsanaṃ, nippariyāyatau pana sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhiyadhammā| addhaniyanti addhānamaggagāmīti addhaniyaṃ, addhānakkhamanti atthau| ciraṭṭhitikanti  ciraṃ ṭhiti aitassāti ciraṭṭhitikaṃ, sāsanaṃ, assa bhavaiyyāti sambandhau| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – yathā yaina pakāraina idaṃ sāsanaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ pavattituṃ samatthaṃ, tatauyaiva ciraṭṭhitikaṃ assa, tathā taina pakāraina dhammañ‍ca vinayañ‍ca saṅgāyaiyyanti|

idāni sammāsambuddhaina attanau kataṃ anuggahavisaisaṃ vibhāvaintau āha ‘‘yañ‍cāhaṃ bhagavatā’’tiādi| tattha ‘‘yañ‍cāha’’nti aitassa ‘‘anuggahitau’’ti aitaina sambandhau| tattha yanti yasmā, yaina kāraṇaināti vuttaṃ hauti| kiriyāparāmasanaṃ vā aitaṃ, taina ‘‘anuggahitau’’ti aittha anuggaṇhanaṃ parāmasati| dhāraissasītiādikaṃ pana bhagavā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai mahākassapatthairaina pañ‍ñattasaṅghāṭiyaṃ nisin‍nau taṃ cīvaraṃ vikasitapadumapupphavaṇṇaina pāṇinā antarai parāmasantau āha| vuttañhaitaṃ kassapasaṃyuttai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.154) mahākassapatthairainaiva ānandatthairaṃ āmantaitvā kathaintaina –

‘‘atha khau, āvusau, bhagavā maggā auk‍kamma yaina añ‍ñataraṃ rukkhamūlaṃ tainupasaṅkami, atha khvāhaṃ, āvusau, paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ catugguṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ ‘idha, bhantai, bhagavā nisīdatu, yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’ti| nisīdi khau, āvusau, bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai, nisaj‍ja khau maṃ, āvusau, bhagavā aitadavauca ‘mudukā khau tyāyaṃ kassapa paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭī’ti| ‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai, bhantai, bhagavā paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ anukampaṃ upādāyā’ti| ‘dhāraissasi pana mai tvaṃ kassapa sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanānī’ti| ‘dhāraissāmahaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni  nibbasanānī’ti| sau khvāhaṃ, āvusau, paṭapilautikānaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ bhagavatau pādāsiṃ, ahaṃ pana bhagavatau sāṇāni paṃsukūlāni nibbasanāni paṭipaj‍ji’’nti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.154)|

tattha (saṃ॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.2.154) mudukā khau tyāyanti mudukā khau tai ayaṃ| kasmā bhagavā aivamāhāti? thairaina saha cīvaraṃ parivattaitukāmatāya| kasmā parivattaitukāmau jātauti? thairaṃ attanau ṭhānai ṭhapaitukāmatāya| kiṃ sāriputtamauggal‍lānā natthīti? atthi, aivaṃ panassa ahausi ‘‘imai na ciraṃ ṭhassanti, kassapau pana vīsavassasatāyukau, ‘sau mayi parinibbutai sattapaṇṇiguhāyaṃ vasitvā dhammavinayasaṅgahaṃ katvā mama sāsanaṃ pañ‍cavassasahassaparimāṇaṃ kālaṃ pavattanakaṃ karissatī’ti attanau naṃ ṭhānai ṭhapaimi, aivaṃ bhikkhū kassapassa sussūsitabbaṃ mañ‍ñissantī’’ti, tasmā aivamāha| thairau pana yasmā cīvarassa  vā pattassa vā vaṇṇai kathitai ‘‘imaṃ tumhākaṃ gaṇhathā’’ti cārittamaiva, tasmā ‘‘paṭiggaṇhātu mai bhantai bhagavā’’ti āha| dhāraissasi pana mai tvaṃ kassapāti kassapa tvaṃ imāni paribhaugajiṇṇāni paṃsukūlāni pārupituṃ sakkhissasīti vadati| tañ‍ca khau na kāyabalaṃ sandhāya, paṭipattipūraṇaṃ pana sandhāya aivamāha| ayañhaittha adhippāyau – ahaṃ imaṃ cīvaraṃ puṇṇaṃ nāma dāsiṃ pārupitvā āmakasusānai chaḍḍitaṃ tumbamattaihi pāṇakaihi samparikiṇṇaṃ tai pāṇakai vidhunitvā mahāariyavaṃsai ṭhatvā aggahaisiṃ, tassa mai imaṃ cīvaraṃ gahitadivasai dasasahassacak‍kavāḷai mahāpathavī mahāravaṃ viravamānā kampittha, ākāsaṃ taṭataṭāyi, cak‍kavāḷadaivatā sādhukāraṃ adaṃsu ‘‘imaṃ cīvaraṃ gaṇhantaina bhikkhunā jātipaṃsukūlikaina jātiārañ‍ñikaina jātiaikāsanikaina jātisapadānacārikaina bhavituṃ vaṭṭati, tvaṃ imassa cīvarassa anucchavikaṃ kātuṃ sakkhissasī’’ti| thairaupi attanā pañ‍can‍naṃ hatthīnaṃ balaṃ dhāraiti| sau taṃ atak‍kayitvā ‘‘ahamaitaṃ paṭipattiṃ pūraissāmī’’ti ussāhaina sugatacīvarassa anucchavikaṃ kātukāmau ‘‘dhāraissāmahaṃ bhantai’’ti āha| paṭipaj‍jinti paṭipan‍nausiṃ| aivaṃ pana cīvaraparivattanaṃ katvā thairaina pārutacīvaraṃ bhagavā pārupi, satthu cīvaraṃ thairau| tasmiṃ samayai mahāpathavī udakapariyantaṃ katvā un‍nadantī kampittha|

sāṇāni  paṃsukūlānīti matakaḷaivaraṃ parivaiṭhaitvā chaḍḍitāni tumbamattai kimī papphauṭaitvā gahitāni sāṇavākamayāni paṃsukūlacīvarāni| rathikasusānasaṅkārakūṭādīnaṃ yattha katthaci paṃsūnaṃ upari ṭhitattā abbhuggataṭṭhaina taisu paṃsukūlamivāti paṃsukūlaṃ| atha vā paṃsu viya kucchitabhāvaṃ ulati gacchatīti paṃsukūlanti paṃsukūlasaddassa atthau daṭṭhabbau| nibbasanānīti niṭṭhitavasanakic‍cāni, paribhaugajiṇṇānīti atthau| aittha ‘‘kiñ‍cāpi aikamaiva taṃ cīvaraṃ, anaikāvayavattā pana bahuvacanaṃ kata’’nti majjhimagaṇṭhipadai vuttaṃ| cīvarai sādhāraṇaparibhaugaināti aittha attanā sādhāraṇaparibhaugaināti viñ‍ñāyamānattā viñ‍ñāyamānatthassa ca-saddassa payaugai kāmācārattā ‘‘attanā’’ti na vuttaṃ| ‘‘dhāraissasi pana mai tvaṃ, kassapa, sāṇāni paṃsukūlānī’’ti hi vuttattā attanāva sādhāraṇaparibhaugau viñ‍ñāyati, nāñ‍ñaina| na hi kaivalaṃ saddatauyaiva sabbattha atthanicchayau bhavissati atthapakaraṇādināpi yaibhuyyaina atthassa niyamaitabbattā| ācariyadhammapālatthairaina panaittha idaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘cīvarai sādhāraṇaparibhaugaināti aittha attanā samasamaṭṭhapanaināti idha attanāsaddaṃ ānaitvā cīvarai attanā sādhāraṇaparibhaugainā’’ti yaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘yassa  yaina hi sambandhau, dūraṭṭhampi ca tassa taṃ|

atthatau hyasamānānaṃ, āsan‍nattamakāraṇa’’nti||

atha vā bhagavatā cīvarai sādhāraṇaparibhaugaina bhagavatā anuggahitauti yaujanīyaṃ aikassapi karaṇaniddaisassa sahayaugakattutthajautakattasambhavatauti| sabbattha ‘‘ācariyadhammapālatthairainā’’ti vuttai suttantaṭīkākāraināti gahaitabbaṃ| samānaṃ dhāraṇamaitassāti sādhāraṇau, paribhaugau| sādhāraṇaparibhaugaina caiva samasamaṭṭhapanaina ca anuggahitauti sambandhau|

idāni (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.152) –

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai  ākaṅkhati vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharāmi, satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaimi, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā…pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kama ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi…pai॰… sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhaumi, aikaupi hutvā bahudhā haumi, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau haumi, āvibhāvaṃ tiraubhāvaṃ tiraukuṭṭaṃ tiraupākāraṃ tiraupabbataṃ asaj‍jamānau gacchāmi saiyyathāpi ākāsai, pathaviyāpi ummuj‍janimuj‍jaṃ karaumi saiyyathāpi udakai, udakaipi abhij‍jamānai gacchāmi saiyyathāpi pathaviyaṃ, ākāsaipi pal‍laṅkaina kamāmi saiyyathāpi pakkhī sakuṇau, imaipi candimasūriyai aivaṃmahiddhikai aivaṃmahānubhāvai pāṇinā parimasāmi parimaj‍jāmi, yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaimi| kassapaupi bhikkhavai yāvadai ākaṅkhati anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhauti…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi dibbāya sautadhātuyā visuddhāya atik‍kantamānusikāya ubhau saddai suṇāmi dibbai ca mānusai ca yai dūrai santikai ca| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati dibbāya sautadhātuyā…pai॰… yai dūrai santikai ca|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāmi, sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāmi, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘vītarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāmi, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ…pai॰… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānāti, sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘sarāgaṃ citta’nti pajānāti…pai॰… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ ‘avimuttaṃ citta’nti pajānāti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai ‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ, tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’ti| iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati| saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhāmi dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai  paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai  sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi ‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā| imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passāmi cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, yāvadai ākaṅkhati dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai, yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti|

‘‘ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| kassapaupi, bhikkhavai, āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatī’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.152) –

aivaṃ navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiñ‍ñāppabhaidai uttarimanussadhammai attanā samasamaṭṭhapanatthāya bhagavatā vuttaṃ kassapasaṃyuttai āgataṃ pāḷimimaṃ paiyyālamukhaina ādiggahaṇaina ca saṅkhipitvā dassaintau āha ‘‘ahaṃ bhikkhavai’’tiādi|

tattha yāvadai ākaṅkhāmīti yāvadaiva icchāmīti atthau| tatauyaiva hi majjhimagaṇṭhipadai cūḷagaṇṭhipadai ca ‘‘yāvadaiti yāvadaivāti vuttaṃ hautī’’ti likhitaṃ| saṃyuttanikāyaṭṭhakathāyampi ‘‘yāvadai ākaṅkhāmīti  yāvadaiva icchāmī’’ti atthau vuttau| tathā hi tattha līnatthapakāsaniyaṃ ācariyadhammapālatthairainaiva vuttaṃ ‘‘yāvadaivāti iminā samānatthaṃ yāvadaiti idaṃ pada’’nti| pautthakaisu pana katthaci ‘‘yāvadaivā’’ti ayamaiva pāṭhau dissati| yāni pana itau paraṃ ‘‘vivic‍caiva kāmaihī’’tiādinā nayaina cattāri rūpāvacarakiriyajhānāni, ‘‘sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā’’tiādinā nayaina catassau arūpasamāpattiyau, ‘‘sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudha’’ntiādinā nayaina niraudhasamāpatti, ‘‘anaikavihitaṃ  iddhividha’’ntiādinā nayaina abhiñ‍ñā ca vuttā| tattha yaṃ vattabbaṃ siyā, taṃ anupadavaṇṇanāya caiva bhāvanāvidhānaina ca saddhiṃ visuddhimaggai (visuddhi॰ 1.69-70) sabbasau vitthāritaṃ| idhāpi ca vairañ‍jakaṇḍai cattāri rūpāvacarajhānāni tissau ca vij‍jā āvi bhavissanti, tasmā tattha yaṃ vattabbaṃ, taṃ tatthaiva vaṇṇayissāma|

navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiñ‍ñāppabhaidaiti aittha navānupubbavihārā nāma anupaṭipāṭiyā samāpaj‍jitabbabhāvatau aivaṃsañ‍ñitā niraudhasamāpattiyā saha aṭṭha samāpattiyau| chaḷabhiñ‍ñā nāma āsavakkhayañāṇaina saddhiṃ pañ‍cābhiñ‍ñāyauti aivaṃ laukiyalaukuttarabhaidā sabbā abhiñ‍ñāyau| uttarimanussadhammaiti uttarimanussānaṃ jhāyīnañ‍caiva ariyānañ‍ca dhammau uttarimanussadhammau| atha vā uttari manussadhammāti uttarimanussadhammau, manussadhammau nāma dasakusalakammapathadhammau| sau hi vinā bhāvanāmanasikāraina pakatiyāva manussaihi nibbattaitabbatau manussattabhāvāvahatau vā ‘‘manussadhammau’’ti vuc‍cati, tatau uttari pana jhānādīni ‘‘uttarimanussadhammau’’ti vaiditabbāni| attanā samasamaṭṭhapanaināti ahaṃ yattakaṃ kālaṃ yattakai vā samāpattivihārai abhiñ‍ñāyau ca vaḷañ‍jaimi, tathā kassapaupīti aivaṃ yathāvuttauttarimanussadhammai attanā samasamaṃ katvā ṭhapanaina| idañ‍ca navānupubbavihārachaḷabhiñ‍ñādibhāvasāmañ‍ñaina pasaṃsāmattaṃ vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| na hi āyasmā mahākassapau bhagavā viya daivasikaṃ catuvīsatikauṭisatasahassasaṅkhā samāpattiyau samāpaj‍jati, yamakapāṭihāriyādivasaina vā abhiñ‍ñāyau vaḷañ‍jaiti| aittha ca ‘‘uttarimanussadhammai attanā samasamaṭṭhapanainā’’ti idaṃ nidassanamattanti vaiditabbaṃ| tathā hi –

‘‘auvada  kassapa bhikkhū, karauhi kassapa bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ, ahaṃ vā kassapa bhikkhū auvadaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā, ahaṃ vā kassapa bhikkhūnaṃ dhammiṃ kathaṃ karaiyyaṃ tvaṃ vā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.149)|

aivampi attanā samasamaṭṭhānai ṭhapaitiyaiva, tassa kimañ‍ñaṃ āṇaṇyaṃ bhavissati añ‍ñatra dhammavinayasaṅgāyanāti adhippāyau| tattha ‘‘tassāti tassa anuggahassā’’ti majjhimagaṇṭhipadai vuttaṃ| tassa maiti vā atthau gahaitabbau| pautthakaisu hi katthaci ‘‘tassa mai’’ti pāṭhauyaiva dissati, dhammavinayasaṅgāyanaṃ ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñaṃ kiṃ nāma tassa mai āṇaṇyaṃ aṇaṇabhāvau bhavissatīti atthau| ‘‘nanu maṃ bhagavā’’tiādinā vuttamaivatthaṃ upamāvasaina vibhāvaiti| sakakavacaissariyānuppadānaināti aittha cīvarassa nidassanavasaina kavacassa gahaṇaṃ kataṃ, samāpattiyā  nidassanavasaina issariyaṃ gahitaṃ| kulavaṃsappatiṭṭhāpakanti kulavaṃsassa kulappavaiṇiyā patiṭṭhāpakaṃ| ‘‘mai saddhammavaṃsappatiṭṭhāpakau’’ti nic‍casāpaikkhattā samāsau daṭṭhabbau, mai saddhammavaṃsassa patiṭṭhāpakau pavattakauti vuttaṃ hauti| vuttavacanamanussarantau anuggahaisīti cintayantau dhammavinayasaṅgāyanatthaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisīti sambandhau, dhātubhājanadivasai tattha san‍nipatitānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ ussāhaṃ janaisīti atthau|
